00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:4: trailing whitespace.
+In the failed insertion shown in Chapter~6, the first camera frame carried the same
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:5: trailing whitespace.
+weight as the last. The attempt lasted 15.233 seconds. From that recording, the visual
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:6: trailing whitespace.
+model took 16 frames and averaged their features into a single description of the
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:7: trailing whitespace.
+action. Reading the frames in reverse order would have given exactly the same
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:8: trailing whitespace.
+description. For an insertion, this matters. A change near the point of contact may be
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:9: trailing whitespace.
+visible in only a few frames, and averaging over the whole action can reduce its
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:10: trailing whitespace.
+influence.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:11: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:12: trailing whitespace.
+The robot also recorded force, torque, and movement during the insertion. These
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:13: trailing whitespace.
+measurements offered a second view of the same event. The main REASSEMBLE experiment
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:14: trailing whitespace.
+first made a prediction from the camera and another from the sensors. It then tested
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:15: trailing whitespace.
+whether combining the two helped identify failed actions. The recorded failure label
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:16: trailing whitespace.
+made that comparison possible, even though the physical cause of this particular failure
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:17: trailing whitespace.
+was unknown.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:18: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:19: trailing whitespace.
+PHM raised a different question. There, the aim was to predict a numerical indicator of
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:20: trailing whitespace.
+gear condition from machine measurements. Photographs taken after a run supplied the
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:21: trailing whitespace.
+target for the main sensor study. A photograph used to define what a model should
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:22: trailing whitespace.
+predict has a different role from a camera frame supplied to a model when it makes a
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:23: trailing whitespace.
+prediction. That distinction affects what can be claimed from the fusion experiments in
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:24: trailing whitespace.
+this chapter.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:28: trailing whitespace.
+In REASSEMBLE, each completed action had one recorded outcome: success or failure. The
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:29: trailing whitespace.
+16 frames from the insertion therefore belonged to \textbf{one prediction}. Treating
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:30: trailing whitespace.
+them as 16 separate test cases would count the same outcome 16 times. A recording could
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:31: trailing whitespace.
+also contain several actions carried out under similar conditions. For this reason, the
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:32: trailing whitespace.
+evaluation kept actions from the same recording together when separating training and
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:33: trailing whitespace.
+assessment data.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:34: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:35: trailing whitespace.
+Failure was the positive outcome in REASSEMBLE, ViFailback, and HANDOVER. In MSM, the
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:36: trailing whitespace.
+positive outcome was an abnormal cutting interval under the label definition used in
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:37: trailing whitespace.
+that study. PHM used regression because its target was a number. A low error in PHM
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:38: trailing whitespace.
+meant that a prediction was close to the indicator derived from the photographs.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:39: trailing whitespace.
+Establishing how closely that indicator measured actual tooth damage would require an
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:40: trailing whitespace.
+independent physical measurement.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:41: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:42: trailing whitespace.
+HANDOVER supplies supporting unimodal and fixed-average results in
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:43: trailing whitespace.
+Appendix~\ref{app:handover}. Its missing participant-to-trial mapping prevented the
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:44: trailing whitespace.
+mandatory participant-safe OOF construction for learned fusion, so the presence of
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:45: trailing whitespace.
+paired modalities alone did not make a fitted stacker eligible.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:46: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:47: trailing whitespace.
+For the REASSEMBLE insertion, let \(p_v\) be the failure probability estimated from
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:48: trailing whitespace.
+video and \(p_s\) the probability estimated from sensors. Both estimates refer to the
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:49: trailing whitespace.
+same action. Their values can differ because the models receive different measurements
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:50: trailing whitespace.
+and learn different relationships from the training data.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:51: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:52: trailing whitespace.
+A classification model produces a score. A sigmoid function turns that score into a
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:53: trailing whitespace.
+value between zero and one. Whether the value is a good probability estimate is a
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:54: trailing whitespace.
+separate issue. For example, if a model gives many actions a failure probability close
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:55: trailing whitespace.
+to 0.8, about eight in ten of those actions should fail if the model is well calibrated
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:56: trailing whitespace.
+in that range. This chapter distinguishes models that combine probabilities directly
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:57: trailing whitespace.
+from models that first convert them to \textit{log-odds}, using
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:58: trailing whitespace.
+\(\ell(p)=\log[p/(1-p)]\). The software clips probabilities near zero and one so this
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:59: trailing whitespace.
+calculation remains defined.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:63: trailing whitespace.
+The REASSEMBLE visual model began with a detector trained to find objects in images.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:64: trailing whitespace.
+RT-DETR has a convolutional backbone that extracts image features at several scales. Its
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:65: trailing whitespace.
+hybrid encoder combines information across those scales. A decoder and detection heads
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:66: trailing whitespace.
+then use the features to predict object classes and bounding boxes
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:67: trailing whitespace.
+\parencite{Zhao2024RTDETR}. For the insertion study, the available label described
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:68: trailing whitespace.
+whether the action succeeded. There was no target box marking the place where it failed.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:69: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:70: trailing whitespace.
+The main experiment used the ResNet18-vd backbone from the
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:71: trailing whitespace.
+\texttt{PekingU/rtdetr\_r18vd} checkpoint. Its weights stayed fixed. Each hand-camera
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:72: trailing whitespace.
+frame was resized to \(640 \times 640\) pixels, and its pixel values were divided by
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:73: trailing whitespace.
+255. The recorded procedure used no further scaling with the ImageNet mean and standard
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:74: trailing whitespace.
+deviation.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:75: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:76: trailing whitespace.
+For each frame, the backbone returned three feature maps. A feature map records how
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:77: trailing whitespace.
+strongly an image feature responds at different positions. The model took the average
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:78: trailing whitespace.
+response across each map channel and joined the results. This gave 896 numbers per
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:79: trailing whitespace.
+frame. Averaging those numbers across the 16 selected frames produced 896 numbers for
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:80: trailing whitespace.
+the completed action. A separately trained classification head used them to estimate the
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:81: trailing whitespace.
+probability of failure. The detector’s hybrid encoder, decoder, and bounding-box heads
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:82: trailing whitespace.
+were bypassed in this experiment.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:83: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:84: trailing whitespace.
+Two details of the insertion disappear during those averages. The first is the exact
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:85: trailing whitespace.
+position of a feature in the image. The second is when that feature appeared. For
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:86: trailing whitespace.
+example, one feature might respond strongly in a frame near contact and weakly in the
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:87: trailing whitespace.
+other frames. Its average could be the same as a moderate response spread across several
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:88: trailing whitespace.
+frames. The resulting 896 values would not tell the prediction head which pattern had
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:89: trailing whitespace.
+occurred.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:90: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:91: trailing whitespace.
+This limitation led to a specific comparison in Chapter~6. The main model averaged
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:92: trailing whitespace.
+frames across the whole action. A later experiment pooled features from the last four of
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:93: trailing whitespace.
+the sixteen sampled frames, giving the final part of the movement its own
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:94: trailing whitespace.
+representation. Both models answered a question about a completed action. Testing an
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:95: trailing whitespace.
+early warning would require an earlier decision time and would exclude every frame
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:96: trailing whitespace.
+recorded after it.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:97: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:98: trailing whitespace.
+Previous studies give reasons to compare these choices. Pretrained convolutional
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:99: trailing whitespace.
+features have been useful in industrial inspection \parencite{Roth2022Towards}, and the
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:100: trailing whitespace.
+Vision Transformer study showed that the amount of pretraining data affects the
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:101: trailing whitespace.
+comparison between convolution and attention \parencite{P010}. Video Swin keeps local
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:102: trailing whitespace.
+information across both space and time, with a computing cost that depends on the clips
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:103: trailing whitespace.
+and image crops used \parencite{P013}. TimeSformer reported an advantage for separate
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:104: trailing whitespace.
+spatial and temporal attention on Kinetics-400 and Something-Something V2
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:105: trailing whitespace.
+\parencite[Table~1]{P034}. The question for REASSEMBLE was narrower: with the available
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:106: trailing whitespace.
+recordings and training time, did retaining a selected part of the action help predict
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:107: trailing whitespace.
+its outcome?
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:108: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:109: trailing whitespace.
+PHM also used a model from the RT-DETR family, but its implementation followed a
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:110: trailing whitespace.
+different route. The software ran a frozen RT-DETR-L detector and collected three
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:111: trailing whitespace.
+intermediate feature maps during its prediction process. Pooling these maps produced 768
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:112: trailing whitespace.
+image features. The software added the time taken to extract them, so the regression
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:113: trailing whitespace.
+head received \textbf{769 inputs}. Any interpretation of this image experiment must
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:114: trailing whitespace.
+account for that timing value as well as the photograph.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:115: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:116: trailing whitespace.
+Keeping an encoder frozen also allowed its features to be saved and reused across
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:117: trailing whitespace.
+training splits. Later experiments investigated what changed when the visual features
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:118: trailing whitespace.
+themselves were allowed to adapt. In ViFailback, the study distinguished a generic model
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:119: trailing whitespace.
+pretrained on COCO, a model previously trained with REASSEMBLE labels, and the completed
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:120: trailing whitespace.
+stages of training on the target task. These models had different training histories.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:121: trailing whitespace.
+Comparing their epoch counts alone would miss differences in batch size, learning rate,
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:122: trailing whitespace.
+image augmentation, and early stopping. Where the record contains only saved features,
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:123: trailing whitespace.
+it confirms feature extraction. A claim about classification or fusion also requires a
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:124: trailing whitespace.
+completed assessment.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:130: trailing whitespace.
+During a REASSEMBLE action, the robot recorded 22 channels: three force channels, three
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:131: trailing whitespace.
+torque channels, two gripper channels, seven joint-effort channels, and seven
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:132: trailing whitespace.
+joint-velocity channels. A brief change in force and a gradual rise in force can have
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:133: trailing whitespace.
+the same average. The sensor study therefore compared two ways of reading the same
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:134: trailing whitespace.
+measurements. One described the action with a small set of statistics. The other learned
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:135: trailing whitespace.
+from short, overlapping parts of the signal.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:136: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:137: trailing whitespace.
+For the statistical model, each channel supplied ten values: the mean, standard
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:138: trailing whitespace.
+deviation, median, minimum, maximum, lower and upper quartiles, range, root-mean-square
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:139: trailing whitespace.
+amplitude, and slope over action progress. Together, the 22 channels produced
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:140: trailing whitespace.
+\textbf{220 features per action}. For a force channel, the maximum retained the highest
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:141: trailing whitespace.
+value in the resampled force channel. The range described the distance between its
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:142: trailing whitespace.
+lowest and highest values. The slope described whether force generally rose or fell as
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:143: trailing whitespace.
+the action progressed. None of these values, on its own, identified the moment when the
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:144: trailing whitespace.
+highest force occurred.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:145: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:146: trailing whitespace.
+These simple features were a serious comparison for the learned model. They captured
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:147: trailing whitespace.
+useful properties of the robot’s movement, and their compact size made them a practical
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:148: trailing whitespace.
+reference with the available training data. Other industrial work has likewise shown
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:149: trailing whitespace.
+that carefully chosen features can compete with learned representations, including in 3D
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:150: trailing whitespace.
+anomaly detection \parencite{Horwitz2023Back}.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:151: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:152: trailing whitespace.
+The second sensor model used PatchTST. To prepare its input, each completed action was
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:153: trailing whitespace.
+represented by 512 positions from start to finish on each of the 22 channels. Position
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:154: trailing whitespace.
+256 was therefore near the middle of the action, whether that action lasted a few
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:155: trailing whitespace.
+seconds or much longer. The spacing was measured in \textbf{action progress}. Two
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:156: trailing whitespace.
+neighbouring positions did not necessarily represent the same number of milliseconds in
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:157: trailing whitespace.
+different actions. This matters if the signal is later interpreted in terms of physical
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:158: trailing whitespace.
+vibration frequencies.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:167: trailing whitespace.
+\displaynote{One schematic REASSEMBLE sensor channel is shown from an action resampled
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:168: trailing whitespace.
+to 512 positions across 22 channels. Patches 1--32 and 17--48 share positions 17--32;
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:169: trailing whitespace.
+their starts are 16 positions apart. Each patch becomes a separate Transformer input,
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:170: trailing whitespace.
+called a \textit{token}.}
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:173: trailing whitespace.
+The original PatchTST model processes each channel separately and shares its learned
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:174: trailing whitespace.
+weights across channels \parencite{Nie2023PatchTST}. This lets it learn from
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:175: trailing whitespace.
+neighbouring measurements without treating every single measurement as a separate token.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:176: trailing whitespace.
+The published model addressed forecasting. In REASSEMBLE, the encoder was trained from
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:177: trailing whitespace.
+scratch to classify the outcome of a completed action.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:178: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:179: trailing whitespace.
+The REASSEMBLE model used a patch width of 32, a stride of 16, a hidden width of 32, two
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:180: trailing whitespace.
+Transformer layers, four attention heads, and a feed-forward width of 64. With no
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:181: trailing whitespace.
+padding, 512 positions give 31 patches. The number actually processed depends on how the
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:182: trailing whitespace.
+software handles the end of the sequence. After encoding, the model averaged the patch
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:183: trailing whitespace.
+features, joined the channel representations, and projected the result to 128 values.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:184: trailing whitespace.
+The inputs had already been standardised before entering the model, so internal scaling
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:185: trailing whitespace.
+was switched off. The software also received a mask identifying observed values. The
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:186: trailing whitespace.
+effect of a missing value depends on where the model actually applies that mask.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:187: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:188: trailing whitespace.
+PHM used a separate patch-based model for minute-level sequences of different lengths.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:189: trailing whitespace.
+Its default patches were 16 positions wide and started eight positions apart. A patch
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:190: trailing whitespace.
+was accepted if it contained at least one valid position. The model then used a mask
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:191: trailing whitespace.

00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:192: trailing whitespace.
+patch grid. Its effect on the reported results has not been measured. An optional output
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:193: trailing whitespace.

00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:195: trailing whitespace.

00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:240: trailing whitespace.
+In REASSEMBLE, an insertion and a placement did not carry the same risk of failure. The
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:241: trailing whitespace.
+retained data contained 243 failures in 1,156 insertions and 22 failures in 1,088
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:242: trailing whitespace.
+placements (Chapter~6). A predictor that knew only the action type could therefore
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:243: trailing whitespace.
+assign a higher failure score to an insertion before using a single frame or force
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:244: trailing whitespace.
+reading. This is why an action-only reference matters. It measures how much can already
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:245: trailing whitespace.
+be predicted from the task the robot was asked to perform.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:247: trailing whitespace.
+PHM needed a different reference. For a held-out gear run, the constant model returned
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:248: trailing whitespace.
+the mean target value from the training runs. A sensor model had to improve on that
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:249: trailing whitespace.
+estimate to show that its readings helped predict the photographic target. The training
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:250: trailing whitespace.
+mean has a specific mathematical property: among constant predictions, it gives the
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:251: trailing whitespace.
+smallest squared error on the training data. The median has the corresponding property
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:252: trailing whitespace.
+for absolute error. The main PHM experiment used the mean.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:265: trailing whitespace.
+Training mean & Do PHM measurements predict the gear-condition proxy better than the
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:266: trailing whitespace.
+same fixed value for every run? \\
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:267: trailing whitespace.
+Class frequency or action context & How much can already be predicted from the training
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:268: trailing whitespace.
+failure rate or the type of action? \\
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:269: trailing whitespace.
+Ridge or logistic regression & What can a regularised model learn from compact features?
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:270: trailing whitespace.
+Ridge predicts a number. Logistic regression predicts a binary outcome. \\
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:271: trailing whitespace.
+Single-input neural head & How well does the camera or temporal sensor model perform on
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:272: trailing whitespace.
+its own on the assessment cases? \\
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:273: trailing whitespace.
+Histogram gradient boosting & Does the result also hold against a nonlinear sensor
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:274: trailing whitespace.

00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:279: trailing whitespace.
+The main REASSEMBLE sensor predictor used logistic regression on the 220 statistics. It
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:280: trailing whitespace.
+learned a coefficient for each feature and an intercept. The study selected its
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:281: trailing whitespace.
+regularisation setting from 0.1, 1, and 10 within the training partitions. The F2 fusion
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:282: trailing whitespace.
+model also used logistic regression, but its regularisation setting was fixed at 1. Only
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:283: trailing whitespace.
+the sensor model selected among those three values.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:284: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:285: trailing whitespace.
+The visual head and the PatchTST classifier used class-weighted binary cross-entropy.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:286: trailing whitespace.
+This allowed failures and successes to carry different weights during fitting. The
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:287: trailing whitespace.
+original context gates used the unweighted form of the loss. The visual head had 114,945
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:288: trailing whitespace.
+trainable parameters above the frozen backbone. The PatchTST sensor model had 109,505,
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:289: trailing whitespace.
+including 90,369 in its projection and classifier. Although the totals were close, the
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:290: trailing whitespace.
+visual backbone had already been pretrained, whereas the temporal encoder had to learn
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:291: trailing whitespace.
+from the study data.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:292: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:293: trailing whitespace.
+ViFailback and MSM also used histogram gradient boosting as a sensor reference. This
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:294: trailing whitespace.
+method combines decision trees and can learn relationships that a linear model misses.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:295: trailing whitespace.
+ViFailback selected a candidate through its validation procedure. The MSM result came
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:296: trailing whitespace.
+from its own controller-feature protocol. Appendix~\ref{app:implementation} records the
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:297: trailing whitespace.
+settings for each study.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:301: trailing whitespace.
+For the REASSEMBLE insertion, the main fusion models received just two numbers from the
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:302: trailing whitespace.
+prediction branches: the calibrated camera probability \(p_v\) and the calibrated sensor
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:303: trailing whitespace.
+probability \(p_s\). By this point, the camera branch had already averaged the frames,
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:304: trailing whitespace.
+and the sensor branch had already reduced the signals to 220 statistics. A fusion model
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:305: trailing whitespace.
+could change how much it used each prediction. It could not go back to a particular
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:306: trailing whitespace.
+frame or inspect the force reading at the moment of contact.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:315: trailing whitespace.
+\displaynote{The 16 camera frames produced one calibrated prediction. Logistic
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:316: trailing whitespace.
+regression on the 220 sensor statistics produced the other. F2 used the two
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:317: trailing whitespace.
+probabilities directly. F4--F6 converted them to log-odds and combined them using
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:318: trailing whitespace.
+context. PatchTST was evaluated separately as a sensor-only model.}
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:324: trailing whitespace.
+PHM combined information at an earlier stage. Its \texttt{fused\_all\_three} model
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:325: trailing whitespace.
+joined learned features from three groups: process context, root-mean-square vibration
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:326: trailing whitespace.
+measurements, and condition indicators. One regression head then predicted the target
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:327: trailing whitespace.
+from the joined features. The software class was called \texttt{LateFusionRegressor},
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:328: trailing whitespace.
+but the values being joined were features. All three groups came from sensor or process
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:329: trailing whitespace.
+data. The photographs supplied the target for this comparison. Also, joining features
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:330: trailing whitespace.
+can increase the number of trainable parameters, so a change in error may reflect both
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:331: trailing whitespace.
+the extra information and the larger model. \textit{Implementation record:
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:332: trailing whitespace.
+\EvidenceRef{E004}{Section~12.4}.}
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:341: trailing whitespace.
+\displaynote{The inputs are engineered machine features. Post-run photographs define the
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:342: trailing whitespace.
+condition-proxy target.}
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:348: trailing whitespace.
+The simplest REASSEMBLE fusion rule, F1, took the arithmetic mean of \(p_v\) and
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:349: trailing whitespace.
+\(p_s\). An increase of 0.1 in either branch raised the combined probability by 0.05.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:350: trailing whitespace.
+The rule learned no combination weights. The branch models and their calibration still
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:351: trailing whitespace.
+had to be fitted on training data, and the calibration of their average still needed to
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:352: trailing whitespace.
+be checked.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:366: trailing whitespace.
+F2 learned a fixed combination from training predictions. It fitted an intercept \(b\),
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:367: trailing whitespace.
+a camera coefficient \(\alpha_v\), and a sensor coefficient \(\alpha_s\):
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:374: trailing whitespace.
+The two coefficients can have different sizes or signs. Within each fitted model, the
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:375: trailing whitespace.
+same coefficients apply to every action. Their values describe how F2 used the two
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:376: trailing whitespace.
+branch scores. They cannot identify which physical event caused an insertion to fail.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:385: trailing whitespace.
+\displaynote{The coefficients and intercept are fixed within each fitted model. Separate
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:386: trailing whitespace.
+assessment folds use separately fitted models.}
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:389: trailing whitespace.
+Training F2 required care because its inputs were themselves model predictions.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:390: trailing whitespace.
+Following the principle of stacked generalisation, the study used predictions for
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:391: trailing whitespace.
+training actions made by branches that had excluded the corresponding recording during
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:392: trailing whitespace.
+fitting \parencite{Ting1999Issues}. These \textit{out-of-fold predictions} formed the
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:393: trailing whitespace.
+prediction bank for the stacker. The outer assessment recordings stayed outside the
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:394: trailing whitespace.
+fitting process.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:395: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:396: trailing whitespace.
+One dependence remained inside the training data. F2 reused the prediction bank for its
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:397: trailing whitespace.
+internal validation. A branch that produced a stacker-training score could therefore
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:398: trailing whitespace.
+have learned from recordings later used for stacker validation.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:399: trailing whitespace.
+Chapter~\ref{ch:methodology} illustrates where this occurred. This detail matters
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:400: trailing whitespace.
+because using evaluation information to select a model can make its reported performance
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:401: trailing whitespace.
+look too favourable \parencite{P004}. The check must cover the whole procedure,
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:402: trailing whitespace.
+including preprocessing, calibration, and the construction of the prediction bank
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:403: trailing whitespace.
+\parencite{P017}.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:408: trailing whitespace.
+A fixed stacker applies the same rule to an insertion and a placement. The context gates
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:409: trailing whitespace.
+tested whether the rule should change from one action to the next. F4 received four
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:410: trailing whitespace.
+action indicators and two input-availability flags. F5 received 36 measures of input
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:411: trailing whitespace.
+quality and the two flags. F6 received all 42 values. For the recorded insertion, F4
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:412: trailing whitespace.
+could use the fact that the robot was inserting a part. F5 instead had access to the
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:413: trailing whitespace.
+measured properties of the inputs.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:415: trailing whitespace.
+For an input \(m\), the gate used context \(u\) to produce a score \(q_m(u)\). The flag
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:416: trailing whitespace.
+\(a_m\) was one when the input was available and zero when it was missing. The gate
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:417: trailing whitespace.
+calculated a weight \(w_m\) for each available branch and combined the branch log-odds:
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:428: trailing whitespace.
+When both inputs were present, their weights added up to one. The original gates had no
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:429: trailing whitespace.
+free final intercept or further calibration step. F2, by comparison, learned an
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:430: trailing whitespace.
+intercept and operated directly on probabilities. A performance difference between F2
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:431: trailing whitespace.
+and a gate could therefore have several causes. To isolate the value of changing the
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:432: trailing whitespace.
+weights, the comparison also had to account for the score transformation, the training
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:433: trailing whitespace.
+loss, and the opportunity for recalibration.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:444: trailing whitespace.
+Earlier fusion methods made other choices about \textit{where} the inputs could
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:445: trailing whitespace.
+interact. Gated Multimodal Units combined internal feature representations
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:446: trailing whitespace.
+\parencite{P006}. Multimodal attention bottlenecks allowed information to pass through
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:447: trailing whitespace.
+shared intermediate tokens \parencite{Nagrani2021MBT}. The REASSEMBLE gates had a
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:448: trailing whitespace.
+narrower task: they weighted two action-level predictions.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:453: trailing whitespace.
+A later experiment began with the fitted F2 stacker and asked whether a small adjustment
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:454: trailing whitespace.
+for each action could improve it. It kept F2’s intercept and branch coefficients. A
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:455: trailing whitespace.
+second network used context to multiply each coefficient by an adjustment:
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:464: trailing whitespace.
+Here \(h_m(u)\) is the adjustment-network output for branch \(m\). Its final layer
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:465: trailing whitespace.
+started at zero, so the model initially reproduced F2 exactly. For a finite output, the
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:466: trailing whitespace.
+multiplier lies between zero and two. The original coefficient keeps its sign. A control
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:467: trailing whitespace.
+model used a constant adjustment, which made it possible to test whether changing the
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:468: trailing whitespace.
+adjustment \textit{between actions} added value.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:477: trailing whitespace.
+\displaynote{Both inputs were available in the clean comparison. Known input loss used
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:478: trailing whitespace.
+the separate fallback described below.}
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:481: trailing whitespace.
+There are theoretical conditions under which adaptive weighting can help
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:482: trailing whitespace.
+\parencite{Zhang2023QMF}. In practice, a quality measure must contain information about
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:483: trailing whitespace.
+when a branch prediction becomes less useful. An observed property of a force signal,
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:484: trailing whitespace.
+the confidence of the sensor model, and the accuracy of that confidence are three
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:485: trailing whitespace.
+different quantities. Calibration can also become worse when test conditions change
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:486: trailing whitespace.
+\parencite{P008}. A later quality-interaction experiment therefore compared prediction
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:487: trailing whitespace.
+heads with matched exposure to altered inputs during training. It was a separate study
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:488: trailing whitespace.
+from the original gate comparison.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:489: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:490: trailing whitespace.
+The difference between a damaged signal and a missing signal is particularly clear in
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:491: trailing whitespace.
+REASSEMBLE. In one controlled test, six force and torque channels were fixed at their
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:492: trailing whitespace.
+training medians. The sensor input was still present. Its summaries and quality measures
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:493: trailing whitespace.
+were calculated again from the altered signals, and the fusion model still had to decide
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:494: trailing whitespace.
+how to use the sensor prediction. In a declared sensor outage, the evaluator already
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:495: trailing whitespace.
+knew the branch was missing. It returned the camera probability and used the camera
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:496: trailing whitespace.
+branch’s own decision threshold. If the camera alone was missing, it used the sensor
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:497: trailing whitespace.
+branch in the same way. If both were missing, it rejected the input.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:498: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:499: trailing whitespace.
+With a declared outage, methods that return the same surviving prediction necessarily
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:500: trailing whitespace.
+give the same result for that action. Handling this case shows that the fallback rule
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:501: trailing whitespace.
+works as specified. Recognising that six \textit{present} channels have stopped carrying
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:502: trailing whitespace.
+useful information is a harder test. Research on ModDrop, EmbraceNet, and missing-input
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:503: trailing whitespace.
+Transformers gives further examples of training for incomplete inputs
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:504: trailing whitespace.
+\parencite{P005,P007,P016}.

00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:540: trailing whitespace.
+run.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:541: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:542: trailing whitespace.
+The original research question also included sound. An Audio Spectrogram Transformer was
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:543: trailing whitespace.

00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:544: trailing whitespace.

00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:545: trailing whitespace.

00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:546: trailing whitespace.

00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:547: trailing whitespace.
+value for these actions remains open.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:548: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:549: trailing whitespace.
+The recorded insertion illustrates the boundary of the chapter’s claims. The model had
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:550: trailing whitespace.
+access to the whole action, so it could use visual evidence near the end. The sensor
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:551: trailing whitespace.
+model had access to the same action through force and movement measurements, but the
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:552: trailing whitespace.
+summaries it received had already lost much of the order of those measurements. The
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:553: trailing whitespace.
+fusion experiment could test whether combining those two predictions helped rank failed
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:554: trailing whitespace.
+actions on held-out recordings. It could not determine the physical cause of that
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:555: trailing whitespace.
+insertion failure or the earliest time at which the robot could have detected it.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:556: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:557: trailing whitespace.
+The later experiments changed individual parts of that setup. Pooling the last four
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:558: trailing whitespace.
+sampled frames changed which frames contributed to the camera prediction. Longer
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:559: trailing whitespace.
+temporal training changed the opportunity given to the sensor model. Anchored adjustment
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:560: trailing whitespace.
+changed the fusion rule. The thesis did not run all three changes together. Each result
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:561: trailing whitespace.
+therefore belongs to the configuration that produced it. Chapter~\ref{ch:methodology}
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:562: trailing whitespace.
+describes the recording splits, training decisions, and assessment conditions needed to
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/03_foundations.tex:563: trailing whitespace.
+read those results correctly.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:4: trailing whitespace.
+Of the 4,530 completed robot actions in REASSEMBLE, 509 ended in failure. A model that
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:5: trailing whitespace.
+predicted success every time would therefore be correct on almost 89\% of the actions.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:6: trailing whitespace.
+It would also miss every failure. For this study, a more useful question was whether the
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:7: trailing whitespace.
+model gave higher failure scores to the actions that actually failed. To test what the
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:8: trailing whitespace.
+camera added, the camera–sensor model and the sensor-only model had to predict the
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:9: trailing whitespace.
+outcomes of the same actions, with the same recordings held out for assessment.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:10: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:11: trailing whitespace.
+The PHM gear study needed a different test. There, the model predicted a number derived
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:12: trailing whitespace.
+from a photograph taken after a gear run. Its error could be measured in percentage
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:13: trailing whitespace.
+points against that photographic target. The same error could not tell us how closely
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:14: trailing whitespace.
+the prediction matched physical tooth damage, because the study had no independent
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:15: trailing whitespace.
+measurement of that damage.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:16: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:17: trailing whitespace.
+These two cases explain the main design choice in this thesis. Each comparison starts
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:18: trailing whitespace.
+with the physical event, the target attached to it, and the information available when
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:19: trailing whitespace.
+the model makes its prediction. The choice of data split and performance measure follows
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:20: trailing whitespace.
+from those decisions.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:25: trailing whitespace.
+REASSEMBLE was the main study of multimodal fusion. Its hand camera and machine sensors
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:26: trailing whitespace.
+recorded the same robotic actions, and each completed action had a recorded outcome.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:27: trailing whitespace.
+This made it possible to ask three questions on the same cases. How well could the
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:28: trailing whitespace.
+camera predict failure on its own? How well could the sensors predict it? Did combining
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:29: trailing whitespace.
+the two improve on the stronger individual predictor?
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:30: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:31: trailing whitespace.
+The other studies tested related questions under different conditions. PHM established
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:32: trailing whitespace.
+target-validity and modality-correspondence limits while combining sensor groups to
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:33: trailing whitespace.
+predict a photographic proxy. MSM supplied the primary completed external manufacturing
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:34: trailing whitespace.
+comparison: locally fitted visual, sensor and fusion models, six target/grouping
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:35: trailing whitespace.
+sensitivities, and controlled corruption tests. ViFailback added task-disjoint robotic
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:36: trailing whitespace.
+assessment and transfer of a specified REASSEMBLE-learned visual representation; target-CNN
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:37: trailing whitespace.
+adaptation remained partial and its corruption study was unexecuted. HANDOVER's official
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:38: trailing whitespace.
+test split supported unimodal models and fixed averaging, while missing participant
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:39: trailing whitespace.
+identities prevented the required participant-safe OOF fits for learned fusion. That
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:40: trailing whitespace.
+supporting study is retained in Appendix~\ref{app:handover}.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:41: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:42: trailing whitespace.
+The five research requirements guided how these tests were read. \textbf{R1} concerned
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:43: trailing whitespace.
+the target and the pairing of observations. \textbf{R2} concerned how each input was
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:44: trailing whitespace.
+represented. \textbf{R3} asked what fusion added over a strong single input. \textbf{R4}
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:45: trailing whitespace.
+concerned the response to missing or damaged inputs. \textbf{R5} concerned the strength
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:46: trailing whitespace.
+of the evidence, including the choice of test groups, the uncertainty of the results,
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:47: trailing whitespace.
+and the cost of the method.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:48: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:49: trailing whitespace.
+The work was exploratory. For example, the first REASSEMBLE results helped determine
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:50: trailing whitespace.
+which sensor representation entered the main fusion comparison. Later tests examined
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:51: trailing whitespace.
+longer training, different camera frames, and changes to the fusion rule. These
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:52: trailing whitespace.
+experiments answer questions raised by the earlier work. They share part of its history
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:53: trailing whitespace.
+and, in several cases, its assessment recordings.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:62: trailing whitespace.
+\displaynote{The original proposal concerned welding. Checks on the available recordings
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:63: trailing whitespace.
+limited the planned independent test. PHM, already under way at the renewed welding
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:64: trailing whitespace.
+audit, provided a study of target construction and sensor prediction, while REASSEMBLE
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:65: trailing whitespace.
+provided the main comparison of camera–sensor fusion. MSM, ViFailback, and HANDOVER
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:66: trailing whitespace.
+tested related methods on their own targets. Each result in the figure refers to a
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:67: trailing whitespace.
+comparison within that study.}
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:73: trailing whitespace.
+A dataset needed more than a video folder and a sensor file. The study needed to
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:74: trailing whitespace.
+identify the event both files described, the outcome assigned to that event, and the
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:75: trailing whitespace.
+group to which it belonged. It also needed enough usable observations of the relevant
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:76: trailing whitespace.
+outcomes to train and assess the proposed models. A dataset could support one part of
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:77: trailing whitespace.
+the research even when another part remained out of reach.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:78: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:79: trailing whitespace.
+HANDOVER is one example. The published trial split made it possible to fit individual
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:80: trailing whitespace.
+predictors and assess their fixed average on the official test trials. Fitting a more
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:81: trailing whitespace.
+complex combination would also have required suitable groups \emph{inside} the training
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:82: trailing whitespace.
+data. The available records did not link participants to trials in enough detail to
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:83: trailing whitespace.
+construct those groups. The study therefore kept the comparison that the data supported.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:84: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:85: trailing whitespace.
+The welding proposal came first. PHM was already under way when a renewed audit of Intel
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:86: trailing whitespace.
+Welding identified further limits on the planned test. REASSEMBLE later became the main
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:87: trailing whitespace.
+setting for comparing visual features, sensor features, and fusion methods. Follow-up
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:88: trailing whitespace.
+studies addressed questions that emerged during this work. The October research record

00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:182: trailing whitespace.

00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:183: trailing whitespace.

00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:185: trailing whitespace.

00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:186: trailing whitespace.

00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:189: trailing whitespace.

00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:190: trailing whitespace.
+participant-to-trial records were insufficient to reconstruct that separation
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:191: trailing whitespace.
+independently or to make new participant-level folds within training. This is why the
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:192: trailing whitespace.
+official comparison remained usable while the planned learned stacker had to be left
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:193: trailing whitespace.
+out.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:197: trailing whitespace.
+Information can enter a model before the final training step. Consider sensor scaling. A
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:198: trailing whitespace.
+model may express each reading as a distance from the average training value. If that
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:199: trailing whitespace.

00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:200: trailing whitespace.
