00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:201: trailing whitespace.
+features, tuning a model, adjusting probabilities, or choosing a decision threshold.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:202: trailing whitespace.
+These steps need to use the development data allowed for that split \parencite{P017}.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:203: trailing whitespace.
+Reusing evaluation results to select a configuration can make its reported performance
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:204: trailing whitespace.
+look too favourable \parencite{P004}.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:206: trailing whitespace.
+The separate PHM four-arm diagnostic illustrates this risk;
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:207: trailing whitespace.
+Section~\ref{sec:phm-diagnostics} distinguishes its shared normalizer from the
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:208: trailing whitespace.
+fold-local preprocessing of the principal sensor comparison.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:210: trailing whitespace.
+A frozen image encoder presents a different case. If its weights were learned from an
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:211: trailing whitespace.
+external dataset and its feature extraction uses no target labels or statistics from the
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:212: trailing whitespace.
+assessment population, the fixed image features can be saved in advance. Later steps
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:213: trailing whitespace.
+that \emph{learn} from those features still have to respect the training split.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:217: trailing whitespace.
+The REASSEMBLE stacker learned from two numbers for each training action: a camera score
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:218: trailing whitespace.
+and a sensor score. It needed scores like the ones it would receive for an unfamiliar
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:219: trailing whitespace.
+recording. A score from a branch that had already trained on the same recording could
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:220: trailing whitespace.
+give the stacker an unrealistically easy example.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:221: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:222: trailing whitespace.
+The study first set aside the recordings for the outer assessment. Within the remaining
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:223: trailing whitespace.
+recordings, the camera and sensor models were fitted on some groups and used to predict
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:224: trailing whitespace.
+actions from other groups. Repeating this process produced a bank of \emph{out-of-fold
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:225: trailing whitespace.
+predictions}. For each row in the bank, the model that produced the prediction had
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:226: trailing whitespace.
+excluded that row’s recording. These scores supported training-side calibration, model
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:227: trailing whitespace.
+selection, and fusion fitting according to the procedure being tested
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:228: trailing whitespace.
+\parencite{Ting1999Issues}.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:229: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:230: trailing whitespace.
+One detail limits how independently the stacker itself was validated. Its internal
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:231: trailing whitespace.
+validation used the same prediction bank. A branch that produced a score for a
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:232: trailing whitespace.
+stacker-training row could have learned from a recording later used to validate the
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:233: trailing whitespace.
+stacker. This dependence occurred \textbf{inside the outer training population}. The
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:234: trailing whitespace.
+outer assessment recordings stayed outside fitting and entered only when the selected
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:235: trailing whitespace.
+system was scored.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:244: trailing whitespace.
+\displaynote{The development lane shows how grouped inner folds supplied the camera and
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:245: trailing whitespace.
+sensor scores used to fit the stacker. Its internal validation reused that bank. The
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:246: trailing whitespace.
+assessment lane shows where the held-out recordings first entered the procedure: at
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:247: trailing whitespace.
+final scoring.}
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:254: trailing whitespace.
+Assessment identities take precedence over shorthand metadata. The REASSEMBLE
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:255: trailing whitespace.
+follow-ups include a previously examined 753-action subset from 26 recordings;
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:256: trailing whitespace.
+Section~\ref{sec:reassemble-followups} and Appendix~\ref{app:supplementary-results}
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:257: trailing whitespace.
+retain the reconciliation and study-specific controls. Sharing assessment cases permits
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:258: trailing whitespace.
+paired comparisons, but does not make an exploratory follow-up a fresh external test.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:263: trailing whitespace.
+Fitting a model, choosing a model, adjusting its probabilities, and choosing a decision
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:264: trailing whitespace.
+threshold were separate steps. In REASSEMBLE, for example, training the sensor predictor
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:265: trailing whitespace.
+determined how its features contributed to its score. Selecting its regularisation
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:266: trailing whitespace.
+setting determined which fitted version went forward. A calibrator then used
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:267: trailing whitespace.
+training-side predictions and outcomes to adjust the probabilities. Finally, a threshold
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:268: trailing whitespace.
+converted each probability into a success or failure decision.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:269: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:270: trailing whitespace.
+The main REASSEMBLE branch calibrators used sigmoid functions fitted to selected inner
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:271: trailing whitespace.
+out-of-fold scores. Calibration can improve the meaning of a probability estimate, but
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:272: trailing whitespace.
+the improvement needs to be checked on assessment data \parencite{Guo2017Calibration}.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:273: trailing whitespace.
+The F2 stacker then fitted a logistic combination of the two branch probabilities with
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:274: trailing whitespace.
+its own intercept. There was no further calibration step after F2. The original context
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:275: trailing whitespace.
+gates combined branch log-odds using weights that added up to one. They had neither a
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:276: trailing whitespace.
+free final intercept nor a separate calibrator after the gate.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:277: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:278: trailing whitespace.
+This difference matters when interpreting the gain from fusion. Suppose a sensor-only
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:279: trailing whitespace.
+adjustment raises some probabilities while keeping all actions within one fold in the
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:280: trailing whitespace.
+same order. The ranking within that fold stays the same. A second fold, however, has its
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:281: trailing whitespace.
+own fitted adjustment. When the two lists are joined, a failure in the second fold can
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:282: trailing whitespace.
+move above a success in the first. The \textbf{pooled} ranking can therefore change even
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:283: trailing whitespace.
+though the order within each fold stays fixed.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:284: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:285: trailing whitespace.
+In the main REASSEMBLE comparison, F2 improved pooled failure ranking over the original
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:286: trailing whitespace.
+statistical sensor predictor. F2 received both a camera score and an extra fitted step
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:287: trailing whitespace.
+for combining scores. A sensor-only model given the same opportunity for second-stage
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:288: trailing whitespace.
+fitting would help separate those effects. The original comparison had no independently
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:289: trailing whitespace.
+refitted control of that kind. Chapter~6 examines the saved predictions to see what the
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:290: trailing whitespace.
+fitted terms contributed. The complete gain cannot be assigned to the camera alone.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:291: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:292: trailing whitespace.
+For binary decisions, the recorded procedure examined thresholds from 0.01 to 0.99 using
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:293: trailing whitespace.
+permitted training-side predictions and a stated objective, such as balanced accuracy. A
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:294: trailing whitespace.
+score at or above the selected threshold counted as positive. This produced a
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:295: trailing whitespace.
+reproducible decision for the experiment. A factory alarm would need additional
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:296: trailing whitespace.
+information about the cost of a missed failure, a false alarm, or an unnecessary stop.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:303: trailing whitespace.
+In PHM, \textbf{mean absolute error} gives the average size of the difference between a
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:304: trailing whitespace.
+predicted value and the photographic target. \textbf{Root mean squared error} gives
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:305: trailing whitespace.
+greater weight to larger errors. Both retain the target’s units. The coefficient of
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:306: trailing whitespace.
+determination, \(R^2\), compares prediction error with the variation in the observed
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:307: trailing whitespace.
+targets. It can become negative, and it can change sharply when a held-out group
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:308: trailing whitespace.
+contains little target variation. Tooth-level and run-level errors were therefore
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:309: trailing whitespace.
+reported at their own levels.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:310: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:311: trailing whitespace.
+For REASSEMBLE failure classification, \emph{precision} asks how many predicted failures
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:312: trailing whitespace.
+were actual failures. \emph{Recall} asks how many of the actual failures were found.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:313: trailing whitespace.
+Lowering the decision threshold may find more failures while also raising more false
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:314: trailing whitespace.
+alarms. Specificity measures how often successful cases were correctly recognised.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:315: trailing whitespace.
+Balanced accuracy gives equal weight to recall for the two classes, while the F1 score
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:316: trailing whitespace.
+combines precision and recall for the designated positive class.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:317: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:318: trailing whitespace.
+The main ranking measure was \textbf{average precision (AP)}. It gives a model credit
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:319: trailing whitespace.
+for placing positive cases high on its list across different thresholds. The study used
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:320: trailing whitespace.
+\texttt{average\_precision\_score}, which weights each precision value by the
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:321: trailing whitespace.
+corresponding increase in recall. Calculating the area under a precision–recall curve by
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:322: trailing whitespace.
+the trapezoidal rule is a different measure. AUROC also measures ranking, but a
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:323: trailing whitespace.
+precision–recall analysis is particularly useful when the event of interest is uncommon
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:324: trailing whitespace.
+\parencite{Saito2015PrecisionRecall}.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:325: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:326: trailing whitespace.
+The failure rates in REASSEMBLE and ViFailback show why the positive class must be
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:327: trailing whitespace.
+named. In REASSEMBLE, roughly 11\% of the actions failed. A model that gave every action
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:328: trailing whitespace.
+the same score would have failure AP of about 0.11. In ViFailback, more than 85\% of the
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:329: trailing whitespace.
+eligible episodes failed. The same constant-score rule would already have failure AP
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:330: trailing whitespace.
+above 0.85, despite being unable to distinguish any two episodes. For ViFailback, the
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:331: trailing whitespace.
+study therefore reported AP for failure, AP for success, and the mean of these two
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:332: trailing whitespace.
+values. When both classes are present, that mean is 0.5 for a constant-score model. MSM
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:333: trailing whitespace.
+used abnormal cutting as its positive class. These scores answer different questions
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:334: trailing whitespace.
+across the three datasets.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:335: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:336: trailing whitespace.
+Ranking also leaves open whether the probabilities are useful. The \textbf{Brier score}
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:337: trailing whitespace.
+measures the average squared difference between the predicted probability and the
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:338: trailing whitespace.
+recorded binary outcome \parencite{P055}. \textbf{Expected calibration error} groups
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:339: trailing whitespace.
+predictions into probability ranges and compares the scores with the observed outcome
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:340: trailing whitespace.
+frequencies in those ranges. Its value depends partly on how the ranges are chosen and
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:341: trailing whitespace.
+how many cases fall in each. A model can rank failures well and still assign
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:342: trailing whitespace.
+probabilities that are too high. Its probability estimates can also become less reliable
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:343: trailing whitespace.
+when operating conditions change \parencite{P008}.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:347: trailing whitespace.
+For its principal comparison, REASSEMBLE brought together the predictions for all
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:348: trailing whitespace.
+held-out recordings and calculated failure AP once. Calculating AP separately in each
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:349: trailing whitespace.
+fold and averaging the five results would answer a different question, because cases
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:350: trailing whitespace.
+from different folds would never be ranked against one another. MSM calculated pooled
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:351: trailing whitespace.
+abnormal-cutting AP for each training seed and then averaged across seeds. Changing a
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:352: trailing whitespace.
+seed measures variation from fitting a model again. It does not create another
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:353: trailing whitespace.
+independently recorded milling interval.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:354: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:355: trailing whitespace.
+To compare two REASSEMBLE models, the analysis kept their predictions paired. If a
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:356: trailing whitespace.
+recording was selected for a calculation, both models contributed predictions for the
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:357: trailing whitespace.
+same actions in that recording. The core comparison repeated this \textbf{2,000 times},
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:358: trailing whitespace.
+sampling whole recordings with replacement and recalculating the difference in
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:359: trailing whitespace.
+performance. This is a recording-level \emph{bootstrap}.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:360: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:361: trailing whitespace.
+A PHM report used 10,000 bootstrap samples drawn at the level of the gear run. The data
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:362: trailing whitespace.
+still came from only \textbf{20 runs across three gear experiments}. Runs from the same
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:363: trailing whitespace.
+experiment could share conditions. More bootstrap draws make the numerical estimate more
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:364: trailing whitespace.
+stable, but they cannot supply more independent gear experiments. The MSM date-based
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:365: trailing whitespace.
+intervals had a similar limit: only five acquisition dates were available.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:366: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:367: trailing whitespace.
+The intervals describe variation in the saved predictions under the chosen way of
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:368: trailing whitespace.
+resampling. They do not cover every change that might occur after retraining the models
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:369: trailing whitespace.
+or moving to a new installation. The intervals also leave out uncertainty from model
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:370: trailing whitespace.
+selection and repeated use of the assessment data. Cross-validation folds can also share
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:371: trailing whitespace.
+training observations, which limits what their spread tells us about uncertainty
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:372: trailing whitespace.
+\parencite{Bengio2004CVVariance}. Where an interval for a performance difference
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:373: trailing whitespace.
+included zero, the data left the direction of the advantage unresolved. Showing that two
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:374: trailing whitespace.
+methods perform closely enough for practical use would require a separate criterion for
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:375: trailing whitespace.
+how small a difference counts as acceptable.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:382: trailing whitespace.
+A missing sensor and a faulty sensor can lead to different decisions. In the REASSEMBLE
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:383: trailing whitespace.
+absence test, the evaluator knew when a whole branch was unavailable. When the sensor
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:384: trailing whitespace.
+branch was missing, it returned the camera probability and used the camera branch’s
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:385: trailing whitespace.
+decision threshold. When only the sensors remained, it used their probability and
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:386: trailing whitespace.
+threshold. If both inputs were absent, it rejected the case. Fusion methods that
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:387: trailing whitespace.
+returned the same surviving prediction naturally obtained the same result under this
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:388: trailing whitespace.
+rule.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:389: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:390: trailing whitespace.
+A damaged signal presented a harder problem. In one REASSEMBLE test, six force and
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:391: trailing whitespace.
+torque channels were fixed at their training medians. The sensor branch still received
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:392: trailing whitespace.
+numbers and could still make a prediction. Its statistics and quality measures had to be
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:393: trailing whitespace.
+calculated again from the changed signal. Giving the gate quality measures from the
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:394: trailing whitespace.
+original clean signal would have described a different input from the one the predictor
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:395: trailing whitespace.
+actually received.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:396: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:397: trailing whitespace.
+For each controlled disturbance, the important details were what changed, where the
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:398: trailing whitespace.
+change was applied, how strong it was, and which observations were assessed. Blurring
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:399: trailing whitespace.
+the camera frames before feature extraction tests a different fault from altering a
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:400: trailing whitespace.
+saved feature vector. For random noise, the number of noise samples and the
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:401: trailing whitespace.
+randomisation procedure also affect the result. HANDOVER, for example, included
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:402: trailing whitespace.
+controlled sensor noise, visual blur, and known loss of an input. ViFailback’s planned
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:403: trailing whitespace.
+corruption evaluation remained unfinished.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:407: trailing whitespace.
+A gate trained with noisy signals has already seen a problem that a model trained only
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:408: trailing whitespace.
+on clean signals has never faced. If the gate performs better under noise, the training
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:409: trailing whitespace.
+examples may explain part of the gain. Later REASSEMBLE comparisons therefore gave
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:410: trailing whitespace.
+static and adaptive models comparable exposure to corruption during fitting. This
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:411: trailing whitespace.
+addressed one important difference while leaving others, such as model size and
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:412: trailing whitespace.
+calibration, to be considered. The idea that changing weights can help rests on how well
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:413: trailing whitespace.
+the available information identifies an unreliable input \parencite{Zhang2023QMF}.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:414: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:415: trailing whitespace.
+The evaluation considered both the score under damage and the change from the clean
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:416: trailing whitespace.
+score. A model can lose less from its starting point yet still finish below a stronger
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:417: trailing whitespace.
+model. When only the sensors were damaged, the intact camera also provided a useful
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:418: trailing whitespace.
+reference: combining inputs was of limited value if it produced a worse prediction than
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:419: trailing whitespace.
+using that camera alone. The tests support conclusions about the stated faults and their
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:420: trailing whitespace.
+severity. Questions about gradual clock drift, several natural faults at once, or
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:421: trailing whitespace.
+operation in a new factory remain open.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:426: trailing whitespace.
+A reported score can be checked at several levels. With saved predictions and outcome
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:427: trailing whitespace.
+labels, it is possible to check the sample identities and calculate the score again.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:428: trailing whitespace.
+Running the fitted model on its inputs also checks whether it produces the same
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:429: trailing whitespace.
+predictions. Training the model again requires the training data, software, settings,
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:430: trailing whitespace.
+and selection procedure. Each step examines a different part of the evidence
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:431: trailing whitespace.
+\parencite{P009}.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:432: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:433: trailing whitespace.
+The main REASSEMBLE visual branch loaded RT-DETR-R18vd through Hugging Face Transformers
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:434: trailing whitespace.
+and took its ResNet18-vd convolutional backbone. The same library supplied the PatchTST
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:435: trailing whitespace.

00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:436: trailing whitespace.

00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:438: trailing whitespace.

00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:439: trailing whitespace.

+\section{Computing resources and the scope of the findings}
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:472: trailing whitespace.
+The project used HFU server resources. The later ViFailback study recorded a single
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:473: trailing whitespace.
+NVIDIA Tesla T4 with 16~GiB of device memory. Reusing frozen camera features and compact
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:474: trailing whitespace.
+sensor statistics made repeated fitting practical. Dataset acquisition and preparation
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:475: trailing whitespace.
+also required resources: MSM used a bounded download plan for selected laboratory files,
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:476: trailing whitespace.
+while REASSEMBLE later released approximately 66.9~GiB of derived caches, retaining
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:477: trailing whitespace.
+source videos, checkpoints, predictions, and recovery metadata. Rebuilding those caches
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:478: trailing whitespace.
+adds work to a fresh run.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:479: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:480: trailing whitespace.
+ViFailback logged about 303.8 elapsed hours across two GPU controller processes,
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:481: trailing whitespace.
+including decoding, validation, and checkpointing; this was neither GPU kernel time nor
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:482: trailing whitespace.
+total thesis compute. Completing its remaining \texttt{FULL20} matrix was estimated to
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:483: trailing whitespace.
+require another 43 GPU days on the single T4, with 47 days allowed for variation and
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:484: trailing whitespace.
+overhead. This forecast of additional work informed the decision to stop further CNN
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:485: trailing whitespace.
+fitting. It is a resource estimate, not a model-performance result.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:486: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:487: trailing whitespace.
+The implementation contribution includes acquiring usable observations, maintaining
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:488: trailing whitespace.
+aligned sample lists, reusing features, and preserving evidence within these limits.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:489: trailing whitespace.
+Scientific findings still depend on the completed comparisons. Limited resources do not
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:490: trailing whitespace.
+establish why a model performed poorly, and an unassessed configuration has no measured
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:491: trailing whitespace.
+result. Head-only timings exclude frame reading and feature extraction; operational
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:492: trailing whitespace.
+timing would also need acquisition and preprocessing costs. Chapters~6 and~8 report
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/04_methodology.tex:493: trailing whitespace.

+\chapter{PHM Case Study: Target Construction, Proxy Validity, and Sensor-Group Fusion}
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:2: trailing whitespace.
+\label{ch:phm}
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:3: trailing whitespace.
+PHM establishes the target-validity problem that precedes the paired vision--sensor
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:4: trailing whitespace.
+studies: machine measurements predict a proxy constructed from post-run tooth
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:5: trailing whitespace.
+photographs. The principal held-out-experiment comparison did not establish lower error
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:6: trailing whitespace.
+for three-group sensor fusion than for a training-mean constant. Photographs supply
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:7: trailing whitespace.
+supervision rather than an inference-time image branch in that comparison. Separate
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:8: trailing whitespace.
+visual diagnostics examine target emulation and appearance agreement without an
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:9: trailing whitespace.
+independent physical-damage reference.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:12: trailing whitespace.
+The study addresses R1 by separating target validity from the correspondence and
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:13: trailing whitespace.
+availability of the prediction inputs. For SQ1, the image and sensor experiments assessed whether frozen visual features and
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:14: trailing whitespace.
+temporal sensor representations could reproduce a specified target. For SQ2, the
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:15: trailing whitespace.
+principal fusion comparison combined context, root-mean-square (RMS) vibration and
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:16: trailing whitespace.
+condition indicators, all available at inference. It did not test joint image--sensor
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:17: trailing whitespace.
+inference. For SQ3, a predictor that already returns nearly constant values can appear
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:18: trailing whitespace.
+insensitive to corruption, so its clean-input error matters. For SQ4, the training-mean
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:19: trailing whitespace.
+comparison asks whether measured sensor inputs improved prediction over a rule that
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:20: trailing whitespace.
+ignores them at inference. It cannot establish whether the photographic target measures
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:21: trailing whitespace.
+damage. That question requires a physical reference independent of the rule.\ev{PHM
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:22: trailing whitespace.
+target and deployment inputs}{\EvidenceRef{E021}{}}
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:23: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:24: trailing whitespace.
+\section{Dataset, Acquisition Structure, and Supervision Challenges}
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:25: trailing whitespace.
+The retained population contains EXP-A runs 1--5, EXP-B runs 1--7 and EXP-F runs 1--8:
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:26: trailing whitespace.
+twenty nominal run units across three experiments. A run can contain multiple tooth
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:27: trailing whitespace.
+photographs, camera-sequence views, high-frequency vibration members, low-frequency
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:28: trailing whitespace.
+context measurements and condition-indicator records. An image, a minute-level feature
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:29: trailing whitespace.
+row and a run are consequently different observational units. Repeated views and long
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:30: trailing whitespace.
+sensor sequences increase the number of stored observations without increasing the
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:31: trailing whitespace.
+number of independently acquired experiments to the same extent.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:32: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:33: trailing whitespace.
+Experiment and run identifiers are retained through derived manifests. Tooth identity
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:34: trailing whitespace.
+and image role define target generation, whereas archive/member identity and measurement
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:35: trailing whitespace.
+provenance define sensor features. Their correspondence is principally at run level.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:36: trailing whitespace.
+This supports joining measurements to a post-run proxy, rather than asserting
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:37: trailing whitespace.
+hardware-synchronized image/sensor events or prospective image availability. Evidence
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:38: trailing whitespace.
+covers these manifests and inspected implementation, with incomplete independent
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:39: trailing whitespace.
+reinspection of raw archive contents.\ev{PHM experiments and acquisition
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:40: trailing whitespace.
+sources}{\EvidenceRef{E020}{}}
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:41: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:42: trailing whitespace.
+\subsection{Supervision and Dependence Limits}
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:43: trailing whitespace.
+The target depends on acquisition geometry, illumination, texture and image-processing
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:44: trailing whitespace.
+choices as well as possible surface damage. In EXP-A and EXP-B, tooth indices 1--4 and
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:45: trailing whitespace.
+5--28 had different image-role coverage. Because tooth identity and view role were
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:46: trailing whitespace.
+confounded, comparing their candidate-area percentages could mix material differences
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:47: trailing whitespace.
+with acquisition differences. The v3 exclusion described below addresses this particular
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:48: trailing whitespace.
+problem; it does not independently validate the retained twenty-four teeth.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:49: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:50: trailing whitespace.
+The organizers reported possible overlap for EXP-A Run-2. A separate analysis addressed
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:51: trailing whitespace.
+equality between derived sequences. A tutorial reports equality of 372 feature rows
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:52: trailing whitespace.
+across 72 derived spectral features for two runs. That is evidence about its extracted
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:53: trailing whitespace.
+representation, not byte-level proof that complete raw archives are identical. Both
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:54: trailing whitespace.
+nominal runs remain visible in the historical analysis. The small number of independent
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:55: trailing whitespace.
+experiments, unequal run lengths and changing acquisition conditions limit broad
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:56: trailing whitespace.
+generalization even when train/test identifiers do not overlap.\ev{Organizer-reported
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:57: trailing whitespace.
+overlap in EXP-A}{\EvidenceRef{E020}{}}\ev{Derived spectral
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:58: trailing whitespace.
+diagnostic}{\EvidenceRef{E037}{}}
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:59: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:60: trailing whitespace.
+\section{Preparation, Exploratory History, and Target-Version Lineage}
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:61: trailing whitespace.
+Preparation distinguished structural eligibility from scientific validity. Source
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:62: trailing whitespace.
+manifests retained experiment, run, tooth and image identities; sensor inspection
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:63: trailing whitespace.
+considered channel presence, units, finite values, temporal coverage and operating-state
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:64: trailing whitespace.
+eligibility. The early EXP-A exploration used runs 1, 3 and 5 and remains a deliberately
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:65: trailing whitespace.
+narrower historical analysis, not evidence that every later included run received the
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:66: trailing whitespace.
+same exploratory scrutiny. Its protected outputs were not rewritten to match the later
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:67: trailing whitespace.
+all-run population.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:68: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:69: trailing whitespace.
+Minute-level representations summarize process and sensor variables instead of passing
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:70: trailing whitespace.
+the entire waveform to the principal model. Timestamp and coverage checks support
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:71: trailing whitespace.
+sequence assembly, while aggregation can discard transients. The high-frequency tutorial
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:72: trailing whitespace.
+has different representation provenance. Duplicate diagnostics, missingness masks and
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:73: trailing whitespace.
+exclusions therefore remain tied to their producing configurations; combining them would
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:74: trailing whitespace.
+define a different dataset from that which generated the historical predictions.\ev{PHM
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:75: trailing whitespace.
+domain implementation}{\EvidenceRef{E034}{}}
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:76: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:77: trailing whitespace.
+\subsection{Target Definition and Version History}
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:78: trailing whitespace.
+The current-end-of-run target is a percentage of a fixed visible-flank region classified
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:79: trailing whitespace.
+as candidate area. It is not remaining useful life, a native failure class, or a
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:80: trailing whitespace.
+calibrated physical spall-area measurement. A run-level target aggregates the largest
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:81: trailing whitespace.
+three tooth values. Raw target values and a causal monotonic transformation are separate
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:82: trailing whitespace.
+outcomes; monotonic processing cannot serve as independent evidence that the raw
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:83: trailing whitespace.
+measurements track deterioration.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:95: trailing whitespace.
+\displaynote{Each row count refers to tooth--run combinations. The run-level target
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:96: trailing whitespace.
+aggregates the three highest tooth values where defined.}
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:99: trailing whitespace.
+Version changes alter the quantity being predicted. Changing ROI geometry and a residual
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:100: trailing whitespace.
+threshold between v1 and v2 altered the proxy scale by approximately a factor of six
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:101: trailing whitespace.
+according to the target-version records. A median-view code state retained the v2 label
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:102: trailing whitespace.
+without a corresponding production artifact. Under v3, a canonical tooth view is
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:103: trailing whitespace.
+selected where available, otherwise the earliest camera-sequence view by embedded time.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:104: trailing whitespace.
+Identical numerical constants in two versions can arise from unchanged top-three teeth
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:105: trailing whitespace.
+and do not prove identical measurement rules.\ev{Versions of the photographic condition
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:106: trailing whitespace.
+proxy}{\EvidenceRef{E023}{}}
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:109: trailing whitespace.
+The visual investigation has three distinct layers: producing a deterministic target,
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:110: trailing whitespace.
+fitting a model to that target, and assessing how closely that target represents
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:111: trailing whitespace.
+physical damage. The first two layers can be inspected from code, saved targets and
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:112: trailing whitespace.
+prediction artifacts. The third requires external reference evidence. Agreement with a
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:113: trailing whitespace.
+heuristic mask is insufficient because it evaluates reproduction of the same rule that
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:114: trailing whitespace.
+supplied supervision. This distinction also applies to the detector trained on heuristic
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:115: trailing whitespace.
+pseudo-boxes: its localization metrics are defined relative to those boxes, not
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:116: trailing whitespace.
+expert-annotated defects. The separate scalar, detector and multitask routes therefore
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:117: trailing whitespace.
+remain different experiments with different outputs and comparison units.\ev{Visual
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:118: trailing whitespace.
+prediction experiments}{\EvidenceRef{E025}{}}
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:121: trailing whitespace.
+Without organizer-supplied continuous damage labels, an explicit and reproducible
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:122: trailing whitespace.
+candidate-area statistic offered an engineering starting point. The implementation
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:123: trailing whitespace.
+measures dark, horizontally extended regions within a chosen flank ROI. Its percentage
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:124: trailing whitespace.
+denominator is the ROI area, so a change in ROI changes the numerical scale even if the
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:125: trailing whitespace.
+same pixels are selected. The term ``candidate'' is substantive: reflectance, machining
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:126: trailing whitespace.
+texture and acquisition artifacts may satisfy the image rule without being physical
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:127: trailing whitespace.
+damage. The target can consequently support a study of proxy prediction and measurement
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:128: trailing whitespace.
+sensitivity. It cannot by itself support a claim about tooth material loss or
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:129: trailing whitespace.
+maintenance necessity.\ev{Candidate measurement
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:130: trailing whitespace.
+function}{\EvidenceRef{E035}{measure\_damage\_candidate}}
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:133: trailing whitespace.
+The inspected target code converts RGB to Lab luminance, applies CLAHE, estimates a
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:134: trailing whitespace.
+smooth background, and obtains a dark residual. Robust standardized residuals define
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:135: trailing whitespace.
+candidate pixels; morphological opening and closing and component-size/aspect criteria
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:136: trailing whitespace.
+retain extended regions. Candidate pixels divided by ROI pixels give the per-view
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:137: trailing whitespace.
+percentage. Historical v2 view maxima and the v3 deterministic-view rule then produce
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:138: trailing whitespace.
+tooth values, followed by the top-three run mean.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:139: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:140: trailing whitespace.
+The PHM scalar baseline invoked the Ultralytics RT-DETR-L prediction path and recorded
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:141: trailing whitespace.
+intermediate maps from three layers. The regression head used 768 spatially pooled image
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:142: trailing whitespace.
+features and one additional value, extraction latency. Latency depends on the computing
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:143: trailing whitespace.
+environment and can confound interpretation of the image-regression result. Separate PHM

00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:326: trailing whitespace.

+\section{Supplementary Diagnostics and Evaluation Sensitivity}
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:333: trailing whitespace.
+\label{sec:phm-diagnostics}
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:334: trailing whitespace.
+Supplementary analyses investigate why the original routes were insufficient, without
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:335: trailing whitespace.
+replacing the principal comparison. The HF/spectral tutorial reports MAE 1.2203 for its
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:336: trailing whitespace.
+full 72-feature model against a v2 constant MAE 0.6797 on eight EXP-F runs.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:337: trailing whitespace.
+Reduced-feature variants performed better than that full model but none beat its
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:338: trailing whitespace.
+constant. Repeatedly inspecting feature subsets on the same test split makes this a
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:339: trailing whitespace.
+development diagnostic, not independent confirmation of the selected subset. Its strong
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:340: trailing whitespace.
+causal language about overfitting or complete archive duplication exceeds what feature
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:341: trailing whitespace.
+tables alone establish and is not adopted here.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:342: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:343: trailing whitespace.
+The four-arm study compares group regressors, concatenation, affine late fusion and
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:344: trailing whitespace.
+context-conditioned embeddings, but fits its normalizer before rotating the folds.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:345: trailing whitespace.
+This preprocessing leak prevents treating it as an isolated LOEO assessment or merging
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:346: trailing whitespace.
+its scores with the principal results. Raw-vibration and tooth-sector screens, AnomalyDINO
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:347: trailing whitespace.
+sweeps, odd/even views, synthetic controls, teacher/oracle constructions and monotonic
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:348: trailing whitespace.
+rescoring address separate uncertainties. An oracle or target-derived synthetic feature
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:349: trailing whitespace.
+can demonstrate pipeline capacity without demonstrating deployable information. The
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:350: trailing whitespace.
+study-specific records retain these diagnostics with their own target and exposure
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:351: trailing whitespace.
+definitions; Appendix~\ref{app:supplementary-results}, Section~\ref{sec:phm-fourarm-supplement},
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:352: trailing whitespace.
+retains the four-arm values separately from the principal comparison.\ev{HF
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:353: trailing whitespace.
