+\section{Synthesis, Limitations, and Motivation for Native-Outcome Paired Studies}
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:357: trailing whitespace.
+Neither the single sensor encoder nor the three-group model improved on the training
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:358: trailing whitespace.
+mean in the observed run-level comparison. The paired interval for three-group fusion
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:359: trailing whitespace.
+included both lower and higher error. Changing the photographic target also changed the
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:360: trailing whitespace.
+constant predictor's error on a fixed split. The comparison therefore challenges the
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:361: trailing whitespace.
+inference that adding sensor representations improves prediction of this proxy across
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:362: trailing whitespace.
+experiments.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:363: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:364: trailing whitespace.
+Optimizing a predictor for physical damage would first require checking the proxy
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:365: trailing whitespace.
+against independent damage measurements or expert area annotations. Until then, the
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:366: trailing whitespace.
+error estimates quantify agreement with the photographic rule. The study also leaves
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:367: trailing whitespace.
+advance warning, remaining useful life and response to natural sensor failure
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:368: trailing whitespace.
+unresolved. Differences in targets, prediction units and preprocessing prevent a direct comparison
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:369: trailing whitespace.
+across all PHM fusion models.\ev{Photographic target versions}{\EvidenceRef{E023}{}}\ev{PHM experiment
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:370: trailing whitespace.
+configurations}{\EvidenceRef{E025}{}}
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:371: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:372: trailing whitespace.
+PHM was undertaken within the architecture programme motivated by robotic welding,
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:373: trailing whitespace.
+rather than as a prespecified feasibility stage designed around its eventual negative
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:374: trailing whitespace.
+result. The subsequent move to REASSEMBLE responded to supervision and comparison
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:375: trailing whitespace.
+limits: recorded action outcomes, visual and sensor inputs at a common completed-action
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:376: trailing whitespace.
+horizon, and more recording groups. The empirical task changed from a photograph-derived
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:377: trailing whitespace.
+gear-condition proxy to retrospective manipulation failure, retaining the questions
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:378: trailing whitespace.
+about representation, fusion and robustness. This progression does not validate a
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:379: trailing whitespace.
+welding classifier or establish that visual and vibration information are generally
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:380: trailing whitespace.
+uninformative.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:381: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:382: trailing whitespace.
+The design lesson is to establish the target, observation horizon and independent
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:383: trailing whitespace.
+evaluation units before optimizing fusion complexity. For PHM, a physical damage
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:384: trailing whitespace.
+measurement would establish what the proxy represents. The error of the constant
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:385: trailing whitespace.
+predictor provides a reference for interpreting model performance under degradation. The
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:386: trailing whitespace.
+subsequent study compares combined pipelines with their single-input reference models
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:387: trailing whitespace.
+and measures their responses to controlled corruption.\ev{Cross-study
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/05_phm.tex:388: trailing whitespace.
+rationale}{\EvidenceRef{E106}{}}
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:2: trailing whitespace.
+\label{ch:reassemble}
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:3: trailing whitespace.
+REASSEMBLE is the central study of completed-action failure classification, comparing
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:4: trailing whitespace.
+representations, fusion and controlled robustness on paired camera and sensor inputs.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:5: trailing whitespace.
+Static F2 improved failure ranking over its statistical sensor component, but combines
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:6: trailing whitespace.
+visual information with an additional logistic fitting stage and did not establish an
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:7: trailing whitespace.
+advantage over equal averaging. The original gates did not establish consistent clean
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:8: trailing whitespace.
+or robustness superiority. Later representation and adaptation studies retain their own
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:9: trailing whitespace.
+controls and assessment populations; their best results were not combined into one
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:10: trailing whitespace.
+model.\ev{Core and follow-up scope}{\EvidenceRef{E044}{}, \EvidenceRef{E040}{}}
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:13: trailing whitespace.
+SQ1 concerns implemented visual and temporal representations relative to engineered
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:14: trailing whitespace.
+sensor statistics. SQ2 concerns early, late and adaptive fusion under shared recording
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:15: trailing whitespace.
+groups. SQ3 distinguishes known missing modalities, synthetic degradation of a present
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:16: trailing whitespace.
+modality and broader distribution shifts. SQ4 concerns the controls needed to interpret
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:17: trailing whitespace.
+these comparisons: native labels, group separation, inner selection, calibration, paired
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:18: trailing whitespace.
+uncertainty and computational budgets. The evaluation therefore includes simple
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:19: trailing whitespace.
+reference predictors and a strong unimodal competitor before interpreting multimodal
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:20: trailing whitespace.
+gains.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:21: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:22: trailing whitespace.
+The task is retrospective classification of an already completed action. It is relevant
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:23: trailing whitespace.
+to monitoring and automated inspection of industrial manipulation, but it is not a
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:24: trailing whitespace.
+direct replication of rotating-machine damage diagnosis. The observation horizon
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:25: trailing whitespace.
+includes the action's end. Consequently, a representation that exploits late frames can
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:26: trailing whitespace.
+be useful here without demonstrating prediction before an unsuccessful event. The
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:27: trailing whitespace.
+dataset paper supplies acquisition context; the retained manifests and implementation
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:28: trailing whitespace.
+establish this thesis's specific population and task
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:29: trailing whitespace.
+\parencite{Sliwowski2025REASSEMBLE}.\ev{Action targets and assessment
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:30: trailing whitespace.
+cohort}{\EvidenceRef{E046}{}}
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:31: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:32: trailing whitespace.
+\section{Dataset, Native Outcomes, and Modality Constraints}
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:33: trailing whitespace.
+The inventory contains 149 recordings; the retained common cohort contains 4,530
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:34: trailing whitespace.
+eligible actions from 148 recordings. A failure is one minus the native success
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:35: trailing whitespace.
+indicator. There are 509 failures, giving positive-class prevalence 0.1124. Failure
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:36: trailing whitespace.
+frequency differs substantially by action: pick has 98 failures in 1,190 actions;
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:37: trailing whitespace.
+insert, 243 in 1,156; remove, 146 in 1,096; and place, 22 in 1,088. This makes action
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:38: trailing whitespace.
+identity a substantive baseline. These filtered counts are distinct from the dataset
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:39: trailing whitespace.
+paper's broader acquisition counts, because eligibility and task construction define the
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:40: trailing whitespace.
+analysis population \citep{Sliwowski2025REASSEMBLE}.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:41: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:42: trailing whitespace.
+The native outcome describes task success, not a spatial defect mask, severity score or
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:43: trailing whitespace.
+verified physical root cause. One recording can contribute many actions, and actions
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:44: trailing whitespace.
+from a recording share acquisition context. Counting 4,530 actions as independent
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:45: trailing whitespace.
+experiments would overstate the amount of independent evidence. Recording identities are
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:46: trailing whitespace.
+accordingly used for outer splitting and uncertainty resampling.\ev{Population and class
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:47: trailing whitespace.
+counts}{\EvidenceRef{E046}{}}
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:48: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:49: trailing whitespace.
+\subsection{Modality Availability and Grouping Limits}
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:50: trailing whitespace.
+The retained modalities are hand-camera images and machine-sensor measurements: three
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:51: trailing whitespace.
+force channels, three torque channels, two gripper channels, seven joint-effort channels
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:52: trailing whitespace.
+and seven joint-velocity channels. These twenty-two channels include operating state as
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:53: trailing whitespace.
+well as direct interaction measurements. Complementarity is thus an empirical question,
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:54: trailing whitespace.
+because both modalities may encode action identity or progress rather than independent
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:55: trailing whitespace.
+evidence of failure.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:56: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:57: trailing whitespace.
+Segment-offset and drift checks could not establish a common timing anchor for audio, so
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:58: trailing whitespace.
+the predictive experiment excluded the audio branch. This is an alignment limitation,
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:59: trailing whitespace.
+not a result showing that an audio model failed to learn. Similarly, seventeen object
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:60: trailing whitespace.
+categories do not establish seventeen disjoint physical objects. The available
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:61: trailing whitespace.
+relationship graph did not support a verified object-instance-disjoint test. The
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:62: trailing whitespace.
+recording-disjoint split evaluates transfer between recordings. Generalization to unseen
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:63: trailing whitespace.
+objects or sites and hardware-clock synchronization remain untested.\ev{Audio alignment
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:64: trailing whitespace.
+and object-group feasibility}{\EvidenceRef{E044}{}}
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:65: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:66: trailing whitespace.
+\section{Cohort Preparation, Alignment, and Evaluation Protocol}
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:67: trailing whitespace.
+Preparation established a common cohort before comparing architecture outputs. Required
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:68: trailing whitespace.
+information included native action boundaries and outcomes, valid camera correspondence,
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:69: trailing whitespace.
+sufficient sensor coverage, and a recording-group identity. These checks support
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:70: trailing whitespace.
+comparable inputs and prevent changing the evaluation population in response to a
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:71: trailing whitespace.
+particular model's performance. They do not certify that every outcome label is
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:72: trailing whitespace.
+unambiguous or that every sensor is physically calibrated.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:73: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:74: trailing whitespace.
+Eligibility checks and representation choices are distinct: temporal pooling changes the
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:75: trailing whitespace.
+view of an accepted action, whereas missing observations can affect eligibility or
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:76: trailing whitespace.
+require a missingness policy.\ev{Preparation and common cohort}{\EvidenceRef{E046}{}}
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:79: trailing whitespace.
+The core analysis uses exactly 4,530 common eligible actions and retains one outcome per
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:80: trailing whitespace.
+action. Inventory recordings and retained recordings are reported separately rather than
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:81: trailing whitespace.
+treating the unused recording as evidence of unexplained model failure. Actions outside
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:82: trailing whitespace.
+the four specified types or without usable required observations are outside this
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:83: trailing whitespace.
+cohort. Exclusion manifests, rather than model-score thresholds, define eligibility.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:84: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:85: trailing whitespace.
+Later studies sometimes assess only 753 of these actions. That population is the
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:86: trailing whitespace.
+original outer-fold-zero assessment subset, containing 77 failures in 26 recordings; it
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:87: trailing whitespace.
+is not a newly acquired independent holdout. Its prevalence, approximately 0.1023,
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:88: trailing whitespace.
+differs from the complete cohort's prevalence. Because average precision depends on
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:89: trailing whitespace.
+prevalence and population composition, a 753-action AP cannot be ranked directly beside
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:90: trailing whitespace.
+a pooled 4,530-action AP as if only architecture had changed.\ev{Core
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:91: trailing whitespace.
+cohort}{\EvidenceRef{E046}{}}\ev{Populations used in follow-up
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:92: trailing whitespace.
+comparisons}{\EvidenceRef{E058}{}}
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:95: trailing whitespace.
+Sixteen distinct frames are selected deterministically at approximately uniform
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:96: trailing whitespace.
+positions within each completed action. The selection uses the recorded action interval;
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:97: trailing whitespace.
+it does not learn where failure begins. Sensor observations are associated with that
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:98: trailing whitespace.
+same action and resampled to 512 normalized progress positions. These positions are not
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:99: trailing whitespace.
+a 512-Hz sampling rate. Normalized progress enables fixed-shape fitting across different
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:100: trailing whitespace.
+action durations while changing the temporal interpretation of features.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:101: trailing whitespace.

00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:171: trailing whitespace.

+\subsection{Gate Behaviour and Fallback Behaviour}
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:526: trailing whitespace.
+No tested original gate/family combination establishes both an appropriate allocation
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:527: trailing whitespace.
+response and a smaller decline under the study's criteria; no supported absolute
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:528: trailing whitespace.
+high-severity AP superiority follows. A gate weight can change with a quality input
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:529: trailing whitespace.
+without changing in a beneficial way. Conversely, a smaller performance decline from a
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:530: trailing whitespace.
+weaker clean starting point is insufficient to establish improved reliability.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:531: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:532: trailing whitespace.
+Exact missing-modality equality follows programmed fallback. It demonstrates consistent
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:533: trailing whitespace.
+handling of known absence, while corruption recognition and useful recovery remain
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:534: trailing whitespace.
+separate capabilities. This is narrower than training an encoder or student to retain
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:535: trailing whitespace.
+performance after input loss, as in transferable missing-modality methods
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:536: trailing whitespace.
+\citep{P005,P007,P016}.\ev{Gate allocation and fallback audit}{\EvidenceRef{E049}{}}
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:539: trailing whitespace.
+The core evidence rejects a broad claim of reliable adaptation for the tested gates. The
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:540: trailing whitespace.
+AP, calibration and intact-branch comparisons capture different consequences of
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:541: trailing whitespace.
+corruption. Robustness to known complete absence is largely architectural here;
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:542: trailing whitespace.
+robustness to unnoticed corruption is empirically weak.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:543: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:544: trailing whitespace.
+Natural missingness mechanisms, simultaneous partial failures, asynchronous clocks, new
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:545: trailing whitespace.
+machines, new operating sites and prospective early warning were not established. The
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:546: trailing whitespace.
+later augmentation study changes training exposure and tests several held-out operator
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:547: trailing whitespace.
+families, but the same recording population and already observed core faults limit
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:548: trailing whitespace.
+claims of independent discovery. An untested condition remains open rather than
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:549: trailing whitespace.
+receiving a favorable interpretation from the model's name.\ev{Tested missingness and
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:550: trailing whitespace.
+corruption conditions}{\EvidenceRef{E044}{}}
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:551: trailing whitespace.

+\section{Synthesis, Remaining Questions, and Bridge to External Datasets}
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:736: trailing whitespace.
+The follow-ups distinguish representation suitability, complementary information and
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:737: trailing whitespace.
+fusion effectiveness. Longer PatchTST training improves AP by .0297 (95\%
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:738: trailing whitespace.
+recording-bootstrap CI [.0122, .0451]) but remains .1441 below statistics. Insufficient
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:739: trailing whitespace.
+training therefore contributes to the initial gap without fully explaining it under this
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:740: trailing whitespace.
+recipe. Yet their same-sensor combination improves on statistics by .0110 [.0054,
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:741: trailing whitespace.
+.0181]. A weaker standalone representation can retain useful complementary predictions;
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:742: trailing whitespace.
+this is not fusion of two sensing modalities.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:743: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:744: trailing whitespace.
+Last-quarter visual pooling raises AP by .0885 [.0424, .1293] with the same fitted head
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:745: trailing whitespace.
+size as whole-action pooling. Additional head parameters cannot explain that contrast.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:746: trailing whitespace.
+Concentrated outcome cues or reduced dilution are plausible explanations, but neither
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:747: trailing whitespace.
+was isolated, and the observation still extends to completion. The selected pooling
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:748: trailing whitespace.
+variant also improves its separate F2 comparison by .0461 [.0260, .0632]. The
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:749: trailing whitespace.
+spatial-encoder and matched adaptation studies show that additional processing or
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:750: trailing whitespace.
+updated weights need not improve the downstream combination; inconclusive adaptation
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:751: trailing whitespace.
+intervals do not establish equivalence.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:752: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:753: trailing whitespace.
+Core F2 includes verified within-fold visual ordering gains, while its advantage over
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:754: trailing whitespace.
+averaging remains unresolved. Anchored adjustment improves clean AP over its anchor and
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:755: trailing whitespace.
+a constant-adjustment control, but lower recall and higher calibration error qualify the
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:756: trailing whitespace.
+gain. Matched corruption exposure leaves the principal adaptive robustness effects
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:757: trailing whitespace.
+unresolved. Changing coefficients therefore does not establish recognition of unreliable
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:758: trailing whitespace.
+inputs. Known absence is separate: the common routing rule fixes the surviving
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:759: trailing whitespace.
+prediction.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:760: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:761: trailing whitespace.
+These interventions were assessed separately on an already examined population. No run
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:762: trailing whitespace.
+combines boundary pooling, the same-sensor hybrid and anchored adaptation. Their
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:763: trailing whitespace.
+strongest individual results do not define an evaluated final architecture. New
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:764: trailing whitespace.
+recordings, natural faults, earlier prediction horizons and uncertainty beyond the fixed
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:765: trailing whitespace.
+fits remain open.\ev{Controlled follow-ups and uncertainty}{\EvidenceRef{E040}{},
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:766: trailing whitespace.
+\EvidenceRef{E042}{}, \EvidenceRef{E062}{}, \EvidenceRef{E113}{}}
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:767: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:768: trailing whitespace.
+The next study returns to manufacturing with native cutting-interval labels. MSM tests
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:769: trailing whitespace.
+whether the representation and fusion procedures remain useful after dataset-specific
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:770: trailing whitespace.
+fitting, and whether the same intact-branch comparisons clarify their robustness under
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/06_reassemble.tex:771: trailing whitespace.
+a different acquisition process.

+\chapter{MSM Case Study: Manufacturing Fusion, Sensitivity, and Robustness}
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:4: trailing whitespace.
+MSM provides the principal completed external manufacturing evaluation of the
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:5: trailing whitespace.
+representation, fusion and controlled-robustness questions. Each prediction concerns a
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:6: trailing whitespace.
+completed cutting interval, with native abnormal cutting as the positive class. Across
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:7: trailing whitespace.
+the tested target and grouping protocols, the logistic sensor branch ranked these
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:8: trailing whitespace.
+intervals better than F2, while quality-gate behavior depended on the input condition.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:9: trailing whitespace.
+The models were fitted on MSM; this study tests applicability in another domain, not
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:10: trailing whitespace.
+zero-shot transfer from REASSEMBLE.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:11: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:12: trailing whitespace.
+\section{Motivation and Research-Question Mapping}
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:13: trailing whitespace.
+Moving from robotic assembly to milling changes the measurements, observation interval
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:14: trailing whitespace.
+and target. The question is whether a camera adds useful information beyond the milling
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:15: trailing whitespace.
+machine's numerical measurements under a new, explicitly grouped assessment.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:16: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:17: trailing whitespace.
+The study connects the original questions in a controlled sequence. The frozen CNN and
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:18: trailing whitespace.
+interval statistics test a lightweight representation choice (SQ1); averaging, original
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:19: trailing whitespace.
+F2 and supplementary quality gates test fusion alternatives (SQ2); controlled input
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:20: trailing whitespace.
+stresses and explicit absence fallbacks address a bounded part of robustness (SQ3).
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:21: trailing whitespace.
+Target semantics, grouped assessment, probability quality and the distinction between
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:22: trailing whitespace.
+software feasibility and predictive value address SQ4. None of these comparisons
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:23: trailing whitespace.
+establishes early warning before an abnormal cut or transfer without target-domain
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:24: trailing whitespace.
+fitting.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:25: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:26: trailing whitespace.
+The pretrained-initialization comparison covered three fixed training seeds and the
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:27: trailing whitespace.
+specified target, grouping and corruption conditions. An initial development run tested
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:28: trailing whitespace.
+the implementation on a small sample. Performance estimates came from the subsequent
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:29: trailing whitespace.
+held-out intervals that met the timing and input-coverage criteria.\ev{MSM experimental
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:30: trailing whitespace.
+design}{\EvidenceRef{E079}{}}
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:31: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:32: trailing whitespace.
+\section{Dataset, Cutting-Label Semantics, and Domain Challenges}
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:33: trailing whitespace.
+The source dataset combines laboratory and industrial metal-cutting observations
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:34: trailing whitespace.
+\citep{Kim2026MSM}. It is a data descriptor, rather than a published
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:35: trailing whitespace.
+unimodal-versus-fusion benchmark. Its streams have different sampling and refresh rates,
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:36: trailing whitespace.
+and the smartphone camera is available only in the laboratory material. The evaluated
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:37: trailing whitespace.
+release is the pinned Kaggle version~2 \citep{MSMKaggle2025}. The retained laboratory
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:38: trailing whitespace.
+material covers VM20i and VMX30Ui; the industrial VF10 component is outside this visual
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:39: trailing whitespace.
+comparison because it does not supply the corresponding video. The source overview
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:40: trailing whitespace.
+describes 56 laboratory experiments. Membership in that inventory is distinct from
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:41: trailing whitespace.
+admission to the final paired cohort.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:42: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:43: trailing whitespace.
+Native labels distinguish stable normal cutting, intervention-worthy abnormal cutting,
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:44: trailing whitespace.
+and stable cutting with a defective tool. Their values are respectively 0, 1 and 2. The
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:45: trailing whitespace.
+primary binary outcome is label~1 against labels~0 and~2. A negative outcome therefore
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:46: trailing whitespace.
+means stable cutting under this definition; it does not certify a healthy tool. Expert
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:47: trailing whitespace.
+annotation uses process behavior, sound and measurements, so the label is an observed
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:48: trailing whitespace.
+process judgment rather than an independently instrumented future-failure endpoint.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:49: trailing whitespace.
+Relabeling every defective-tool interval as abnormal would change the task. The study
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:50: trailing whitespace.
+instead repeats the pipeline after excluding label~2 and retains that as a distinct
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:51: trailing whitespace.
+target sensitivity.\ev{MSM target definition}{\EvidenceRef{E074}{}}
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:52: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:53: trailing whitespace.
+Of 2,512 native labeled intervals, 1,895 satisfy the joint video and numerical-input
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:54: trailing whitespace.
+requirements, including 336 positives. The remaining 617 retain their original labels
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:55: trailing whitespace.
+and explicit exclusion reasons. One prediction refers to one completed cutting interval.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:56: trailing whitespace.
+Neither the whole interval nor its final label is shortened into a prediction horizon
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:57: trailing whitespace.
+that would establish advance warning.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:58: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:59: trailing whitespace.
+\subsection{Visibility and Independence Limits}
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:60: trailing whitespace.
+Milling video can show the machine and process context while coolant, enclosure geometry
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:61: trailing whitespace.
+and occlusion conceal the tool--workpiece interaction. Successful decoding is therefore
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:62: trailing whitespace.
+insufficient evidence that the labeled abnormality is optically observable. A visual
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:63: trailing whitespace.
+classifier may respond to operating context, lighting or experiment-specific appearance
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:64: trailing whitespace.
+rather than the underlying cutting event. The visual training--assessment
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:65: trailing whitespace.
+gap quantified in Section~\ref{sec:msm-clean} is compatible with this concern, but does not identify its cause.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:66: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:67: trailing whitespace.
+The machine stream is also an observation of the process rather than direct,
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:68: trailing whitespace.
+instantaneous ground truth. Aggregated electrical, load and vibration-related
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:69: trailing whitespace.
+measurements can describe an interval while smoothing short events. Their timing and
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:70: trailing whitespace.
+update cadence limit any claim about event onset or control latency. Group identity
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:71: trailing whitespace.
+creates a further difficulty: experiment folders provide a useful split boundary, but
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:72: trailing whitespace.
+they are not verified identities for physical tools, workpieces or sessions. These
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:73: trailing whitespace.
+limits motivate both the technical gates and the stricter grouping sensitivities.\ev{MSM
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:74: trailing whitespace.
+validation limits}{\EvidenceRef{E075}{}}
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:75: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:76: trailing whitespace.
+\section{Preparation, Timing Alignment, and Cohort Support}
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:77: trailing whitespace.
+Preparation admits examples only when both modalities meet a fixed input contract. The
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:78: trailing whitespace.
+dominant exclusion is lack of validated acoustic timing support, followed by rejected or
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:79: trailing whitespace.
+insufficient alignment and numerical gaps. These are technical exclusions, not removals
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:80: trailing whitespace.
+based on model correctness. The exclusion record identifies intervals removed before
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:81: trailing whitespace.
+modelling. Successful decoding alone does not establish that an anomaly is visible.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:84: trailing whitespace.
+The final adapter reuses the corrected Stage~2/5 acoustic calibration rather than
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:85: trailing whitespace.
+fitting timing offsets against outcome labels. Its convention is video time equal to
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:86: trailing whitespace.
+sensor time plus the accepted offset, within the region supported by the calibration. A
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:87: trailing whitespace.
+documented correction matters here: VMX9 uses an offset of $+49.73$~s; the rejected
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:88: trailing whitespace.
+$+16.04$~s alias is not an interchangeable alternative. Treating the two alignments as
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:89: trailing whitespace.
+equivalent would change which visual evidence accompanies each native interval.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:90: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:91: trailing whitespace.
+Cuts use half-open intervals and uncertainty guards. Each admitted interval has sixteen
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:92: trailing whitespace.
+ordered, distinct native frames with source timestamps and content identities. The
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:93: trailing whitespace.
+assessment concerns the accepted support, not every point of the original recording. An
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:94: trailing whitespace.
+accepted offset supplies a defensible correspondence under the recorded calibration; it
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:95: trailing whitespace.
+does not prove hardware synchronization or eliminate residual timing uncertainty.\ev{MSM
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:96: trailing whitespace.
+timing and alignment checks}{\EvidenceRef{E075}{}}
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:99: trailing whitespace.
+The frozen recipe corrects the source sample aspect ratio of $3{:}8$ and uses
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:100: trailing whitespace.
+aspect-preserving, centered black letterboxing to $640\times640$. This preserves
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:101: trailing whitespace.
+geometry instead of stretching narrow process views into a square. Sixteen interval
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:102: trailing whitespace.
+frames pass through the same CNN, and spatial and temporal means produce one interval
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:103: trailing whitespace.
+representation. Frame order is retained in provenance but is not learned by the
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:104: trailing whitespace.
+mean-pooling model.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:105: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:106: trailing whitespace.
+At least sixteen distinct native frames are required; repeating a short clip to reach
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:107: trailing whitespace.
+the nominal frame count is not accepted as additional temporal coverage. Later stress
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:108: trailing whitespace.
+conditions alter actual image content before feature extraction. Neither the common crop
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:109: trailing whitespace.
+nor a displayed context view is a detector box, a tool annotation, or a verified
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:110: trailing whitespace.
+abnormal region. The derived galleries support examination of the evidence and errors;
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:111: trailing whitespace.
+they do not replace independent frame-level annotations.\ev{MSM visual input
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:112: trailing whitespace.
+contract}{\EvidenceRef{E068}{}}
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:115: trailing whitespace.
+The evaluated numerical input comprises fifteen MTConnect channels: power, spindle speed
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:116: trailing whitespace.
+and load, feed rate, axis loads, and the supplied RMS-related channels. Six
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:117: trailing whitespace.
+channel-major statistics---mean, population standard deviation, RMS, minimum, maximum
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:118: trailing whitespace.
+and peak-to-peak---yield ninety values per interval. The channel names and measurement
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:119: trailing whitespace.
+semantics remain those of the release; they are not recast as a new calibrated force
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:120: trailing whitespace.
+signal.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:121: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:122: trailing whitespace.
+Numerical support must contain at least five finite rows, with no gap greater than
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:123: trailing whitespace.
+$0.5$~s including interval edges. The pipeline neither interpolates an unsupported gap
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:124: trailing whitespace.
+nor substitutes an accelerometer branch. Means, standard deviations, imputation values
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:125: trailing whitespace.
+and corruption scales that require population estimation are fitted only within the
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:126: trailing whitespace.
+appropriate training partition. This prevents the assessment distribution from
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:127: trailing whitespace.
+influencing the numerical normalization, while leaving the source stream's aggregation
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:128: trailing whitespace.
+and latency limitations intact.\ev{MSM numerical coverage}{\EvidenceRef{E075}{}}
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:131: trailing whitespace.
+The primary cohort has 45 nominal experiment groups, 17 containing positives, seven
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:132: trailing whitespace.
+machine/date proxies and five calendar dates. Every actual fitting, out-of-fold and
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:133: trailing whitespace.
+assessment partition must contain both classes. Table~\ref{tab:msm-support} records the
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:134: trailing whitespace.
+positive/total assessment support for all six target/grouping protocols. The folds
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:135: trailing whitespace.
+within a protocol partition its admitted cohort; the six protocols are sensitivities on
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:136: trailing whitespace.
+related observations, not six independent datasets.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:151: trailing whitespace.
+\displaynote{Each cell gives the number of positive intervals divided by the total
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:152: trailing whitespace.
+number of intervals. Label~1 is positive.}
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:156: trailing whitespace.
+Experiment and date identifiers serve as proxies for physical independence. Repeated
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:157: trailing whitespace.
+tool or workpiece use across dates remains unresolved. The additional DOWN-only,
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:158: trailing whitespace.
+label-2-excluded comparison fails the fixed class-support requirement. The split seeds
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:159: trailing whitespace.
+and fold counts were fixed, leaving that comparison without an estimate.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:160: trailing whitespace.
+Direction-specific held-out summaries are descriptive and cannot isolate a causal effect
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:161: trailing whitespace.
+of cutting direction.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:164: trailing whitespace.

+\section{Architecture Reuse, Adaptations, and Evaluation Protocol}
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:178: trailing whitespace.
+The pretrained condition (P) used the ResNet18-vd CNN backbone inside the RT-DETR-R18vd
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:179: trailing whitespace.
+checkpoint \citep{Zhao2024RTDETR}. The extractor stopped at the CNN feature maps, before
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:180: trailing whitespace.
+the hybrid Transformer encoder, detection decoder, and box heads. Spatial means from the
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:181: trailing whitespace.
+last three maps yielded 896 features per frame. Averaging across sixteen frames gave one
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:182: trailing whitespace.
+vector per cutting interval. With the backbone frozen, a newly fitted
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:183: trailing whitespace.
+$896\rightarrow128\rightarrow1$ head with ReLU and dropout~0.1 supplied the target
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:184: trailing whitespace.
+prediction. It had 114,945 trainable parameters.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:185: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:186: trailing whitespace.
+The numerical branch is a fresh balanced L2 logistic regression on the ninety
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:187: trailing whitespace.
+statistics, with $C=1$. A separately fitted histogram gradient boosting model provides
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:188: trailing whitespace.
+the stronger sensor control. It is essential to keep that control separate: F2 consumes
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:189: trailing whitespace.
+the logistic branch, not the HGB predictions. Averaging combines the two branch
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:190: trailing whitespace.
+probabilities without a fitted fusion parameter. Original F2 is a newly fitted
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:191: trailing whitespace.
+unweighted L2 logistic model on visual and sensor probabilities generated out of fold.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:192: trailing whitespace.
+No source failure head, scaler, calibrator or fusion coefficient is transferred.\ev{MSM
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:193: trailing whitespace.
+executed composition}{\EvidenceRef{E077}{}}
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:194: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:195: trailing whitespace.
+The clean and augmented quality gates are additional comparisons. They reuse a small
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:196: trailing whitespace.
+softmax gate over branch logits, with label-free image-quality summaries, numerical
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:197: trailing whitespace.
+variability, missing-channel fractions and availability flags. These summaries may
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:198: trailing whitespace.
+encode process context as well as degradation. A changing allocation therefore is not a
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:199: trailing whitespace.
+calibrated estimate of modality reliability. This matters because a reliability-aware
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:200: trailing whitespace.
+fusion argument requires a quality proxy that actually predicts error, rather than a
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:201: trailing whitespace.
+proxy that merely changes with the input \citep{Zhang2023QMF}. QG-clean uses clean OOF
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:202: trailing whitespace.
+views. QG-aug sees clean views plus seven predefined lowest-severity corruptions, while
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:203: trailing whitespace.
+F2-aug is the exposure-matched static control. Clean fitting, corruption exposure and
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:204: trailing whitespace.
+quality conditioning remain separate interventions.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:205: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:206: trailing whitespace.
+R denotes a different question: initialization from a specifically completed
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:207: trailing whitespace.
+REASSEMBLE-learned CNN. The exact canonical source required by MSM's gate was
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:208: trailing whitespace.
+unavailable in the inspected evaluation, so that condition remains unmeasured. This does
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:209: trailing whitespace.
+not contradict the P completion or the later, differently specified ViFailback donor
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:210: trailing whitespace.
+study. Architecture reuse, generic pretraining and transfer of task-learned weights
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:211: trailing whitespace.
+retain separate provenance.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:212: trailing whitespace.

00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:213: trailing whitespace.
+\subsection{Implementation Checks and Bounded Development Run}
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:214: trailing whitespace.
+The bounded development exercise was an engineering check: its two-epoch,
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:215: trailing whitespace.
+at-most-24-interval design restricted head updates and CNN frame forwards and prohibited
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:216: trailing whitespace.
+outer-assessment predictions. It could expose shape, gradient, memory or reload errors,
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:217: trailing whitespace.
+but could not estimate generalization. Generalization was assessed in the subsequent
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:218: trailing whitespace.
+evaluation on held-out intervals.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:219: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:220: trailing whitespace.
+The saved implementation audit checks planned cases, source identities, native
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:221: trailing whitespace.
+assessment units, model budgets and checkpoint reloads. It also refits the fixed F2
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:222: trailing whitespace.
+estimator from saved clean OOF probabilities and reports zero coefficient discrepancy.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:223: trailing whitespace.
+The CNN cache check reports maximum difference zero on an outer-training-only example.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:224: trailing whitespace.
+These support consistency of the saved procedure and its outputs. Their scope is
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:225: trailing whitespace.
+narrower than reproduction of stochastic training: exact reload and metric recomputation
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:226: trailing whitespace.
+cannot alone certify that every training step would recur in another environment.\ev{MSM
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:227: trailing whitespace.
+implementation and reproduction scope}{\EvidenceRef{E078}{}}
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:228: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:229: trailing whitespace.
+\subsection{Evaluation Protocol and Conditions Without Estimates}
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:230: trailing whitespace.
+The completed matrix uses three outer and two inner StratifiedGroupKFold partitions,
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:231: trailing whitespace.
+fixed split seeds and training seeds 42, 123 and 20261023. Inner fits produce branch OOF
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:232: trailing whitespace.
+probabilities for learned fusion; outer assessments remain excluded from fitting. The
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:233: trailing whitespace.
+six target/grouping protocols yield 54 completed case records. The 54 cases include
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:234: trailing whitespace.
+different folds and seeds from overlapping protocols.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:235: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:236: trailing whitespace.
+The main visual head runs for ten fixed epochs and the gate for forty. The assessment
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:237: trailing whitespace.
+uses a fixed threshold of 0.5, with no assessment-selected epoch or fitted primary
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:238: trailing whitespace.
+calibrator. The definitions in Section~\ref{sec:ch4-metrics} apply: AP is average precision,
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:239: trailing whitespace.
+not trapezoidal PR area, and F2 names the stacker. Each seed's disjoint outer predictions
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:240: trailing whitespace.
+are pooled before AP is computed; the three seed-specific APs are then summarized.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:241: trailing whitespace.
+These are not mean-fold APs.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:242: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:243: trailing whitespace.
+Conditions with a missing source checkpoint, inadequate class support or insufficient
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:244: trailing whitespace.
+temporal coverage yielded no estimate. The results below cover the conditions that met
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:245: trailing whitespace.
+the original criteria.\ev{MSM fixed evaluation protocol}{\EvidenceRef{E070}{}}
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:246: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:247: trailing whitespace.

+\section{Metric Trade-offs, Limitations, and Transition to ViFailback}
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:361: trailing whitespace.
+For seed~42, F2 had lower AP than logistic sensing but a lower Brier score (0.0561
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:362: trailing whitespace.
+versus 0.0617) and higher F1 (0.8204 versus 0.8113). QG-clean had higher AP than
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:363: trailing whitespace.
+F2, a worse Brier score of 0.0644 and a lower F1 of 0.7864. Thus the ranking
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:364: trailing whitespace.
+comparison favored logistic sensing, whereas probability error and fixed-threshold
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:365: trailing whitespace.
+decisions favored F2 for this seed. The relative performance of the gates depended on
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/07_msm.tex:366: trailing whitespace.
+the input condition.

+\section{Preparation, Three-View Inputs, and Pre-Checks}
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:79: trailing whitespace.
+Sixteen uniform, matching frame indices are selected in each of three cameras. These are
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:80: trailing whitespace.
+sixteen temporal positions and 48 images, not 48 distinct timestamps. The source image
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:81: trailing whitespace.
+processor resizes to $640\times640$, divides RGB values by 255 and does not apply
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:82: trailing whitespace.
+ImageNet normalization. Equal camera and temporal means remove order information from
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:83: trailing whitespace.
+the episode embedding. A fixed crop shown in an example is context visualization, not a
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:84: trailing whitespace.
+detected object or validated failure location.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:85: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:86: trailing whitespace.
+The sensor adapter retains fourteen position, twelve nonplaceholder velocity and two
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:87: trailing whitespace.
+gripper-effort fields. Ten statistics per channel produce 280 values. These observations
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:88: trailing whitespace.
+are not calibrated wrench or force/torque measurements. Labels, failure categories,
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:89: trailing whitespace.
+action commands, keyframes and task/source identifiers do not enter ordinary model
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:90: trailing whitespace.
+features. Per-episode feature calculation is separated from population fitting:
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:91: trailing whitespace.
+standardizers use T only, and calibration uses disjoint C examples.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:92: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:93: trailing whitespace.
+The twenty-example visual pilot was selected for observability and balance. Nineteen
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:94: trailing whitespace.
+decided observations agreed with native labels; B02 remained uncertain. This is a
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:95: trailing whitespace.
+bounded project examination, not an independent human-rated prevalence sample or a
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:96: trailing whitespace.
+held-out classifier benchmark. The additional automated consistency checks are also not
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:97: trailing whitespace.
+a signed second-human review. Technical decoding and a successful native-label join do
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:98: trailing whitespace.
+not resolve every episode's semantic meaning.\ev{ViFailback
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:99: trailing whitespace.
+preprocessing}{\EvidenceRef{E088}{}}\ev{Scope of independent
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:100: trailing whitespace.
+checks}{\EvidenceRef{E092}{}}
