+\section{Models, Transfer/Adaptation Regimes, and Evaluation Protocol}
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:103: trailing whitespace.
+The original visual branch loaded the ResNet18-vd CNN backbone inside the pretrained
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:104: trailing whitespace.
+RT-DETR-R18vd checkpoint \citep{Zhao2024RTDETR}. The extractor stopped at the CNN
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:105: trailing whitespace.
+feature maps, before the hybrid Transformer encoder, detection decoder, and box heads.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:106: trailing whitespace.
+Spatial means of three maps with 128, 256, and 512 channels yielded 896 features per
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:107: trailing whitespace.
+image. The branch averaged these features across frames and cameras and fitted a new
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:108: trailing whitespace.
+$896\rightarrow128\rightarrow1$ head to predict the episode outcome. Three-view pooling,
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:109: trailing whitespace.
+the sensor statistics, and the task partitions were specific to ViFailback.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:110: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:111: trailing whitespace.
+The logistic sensor branch uses balanced L2 regression with $C=1$, training-only scaling
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:112: trailing whitespace.
+and a separately fitted sigmoid calibrator. Averaging and F2 consume its calibrated
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:113: trailing whitespace.
+probabilities alongside the visual branch. F2 is unweighted L2 logistic probability
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:114: trailing whitespace.
+stacking; HGB is a stronger separate reference and never replaces the sensor input
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:115: trailing whitespace.
+inside original F2. Consequently, a gain over vision or logistic sensing cannot be
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:116: trailing whitespace.
+reported as a gain over the strongest available unimodal method.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:117: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:118: trailing whitespace.
+HGB uses 200 iterations, learning rate 0.05, L2 penalty 0.1 and minimum leaf size
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:119: trailing whitespace.
+twenty; leaves 7, 15 and 31 are selected on V macro AP, followed by C-only calibration.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:120: trailing whitespace.
+Its search is real candidate selection, unlike the fixed MSM recipe or a claim that
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:121: trailing whitespace.
+every parameter was optimized. It remains secondary and does not change the prespecified
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:122: trailing whitespace.
+F2 input after its favorable assessment.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:123: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:124: trailing whitespace.
+\ev{Exploratory HGB recipe}{\EvidenceRef{E094}{}}
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:125: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:126: trailing whitespace.
+The donor arms transferred compatible CNN tensors from a completed source checkpoint and
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:127: trailing whitespace.
+fitted a fresh ViFailback head, scaler, calibrator and fusion model. The FROZEN and
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:128: trailing whitespace.
+DONOR conditions held the CNN weights fixed. The FULL conditions train the executed CNN
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:129: trailing whitespace.
+convolutions and normalization affine terms while holding running moments fixed; their
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:130: trailing whitespace.
+completed coverage is stated below. This is CNN adaptation, not training the detector
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:131: trailing whitespace.
+encoder, decoder or detection heads. The completed DONOR comparison therefore measures
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:132: trailing whitespace.
+supervised target-head fitting on a transferred frozen representation.\ev{Matched model
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:133: trailing whitespace.
+definitions}{\EvidenceRef{E097}{}}
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:134: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:135: trailing whitespace.
+\subsection{Task Partitions, Calibration, and Fitting Budgets}
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:136: trailing whitespace.
+The original protocol uses three outer task-disjoint folds and three inner OOF
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:137: trailing whitespace.
+assessments within each outer-training pool. Each branch separates gradient-fitting T,
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:138: trailing whitespace.
+checkpoint-selection V, calibration C and assessment A task groups. A final branch is
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:139: trailing whitespace.
+not automatically fitted to the entire outer-training pool: its T/V/C roles remain
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:140: trailing whitespace.
+distinct. The inner assessment probabilities build a meta bank without using examples
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:141: trailing whitespace.
+that fitted, selected or calibrated their generating branch.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:142: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:143: trailing whitespace.
+Checkpoint selection uses validation macro AP; the selected checkpoint is then
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:144: trailing whitespace.
+calibrated on C, and assessment uses a fixed threshold of 0.5. Failure AP, success AP
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:145: trailing whitespace.
+and their arithmetic mean are retained. With failure prevalence above 85\%,
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:146: trailing whitespace.
+failure-positive AP alone can conceal poor success discrimination. Pooled AP is
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:147: trailing whitespace.
+calculated from disjoint outer predictions, separately for each seed. It is not the
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:148: trailing whitespace.
+arithmetic mean of fold APs, and seed repetition does not add independent episodes.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:149: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:150: trailing whitespace.
+The later matched twenty-epoch arms use the first twenty epochs of a fixed cosine-50
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:151: trailing whitespace.
+schedule, while the hundred-epoch arms use cosine-100. Highest V macro AP selects the
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:152: trailing whitespace.
+checkpoint, then lower V BCE and the earliest epoch resolve ties. All configured epochs
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:153: trailing whitespace.
+are intended to execute even when the chosen checkpoint is earlier. The twenty-epoch
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:154: trailing whitespace.
+study has a complete three-inner OOF bank. The hundred-epoch pilot instead reserves 788
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:155: trailing whitespace.
+meta episodes under one predeclared role, followed by 1,107 outer assessments. It is not
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:156: trailing whitespace.
+a full nested three-fold hundred-epoch experiment.\ev{Original evaluation
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:157: trailing whitespace.
+contract}{\EvidenceRef{E094}{}}\ev{Matched training contract}{\EvidenceRef{E098}{}}
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:158: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:159: trailing whitespace.
+\section{Empirical Investigations}
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:160: trailing whitespace.
+The original study evaluated the frozen visual branch, its high-camera control, sensor
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:161: trailing whitespace.
+models and clean fusion. By 24 November, the matched study had 79 completed fits of 111
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:162: trailing whitespace.
+planned: 36 each for FROZEN20 and DONOR20, four for FULL20, and one for each
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:163: trailing whitespace.
+hundred-epoch pilot. These support 22 outer fusion assessments: eighteen frozen/donor
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:164: trailing whitespace.
+twenty-epoch assessments, one FULL20 assessment and three pilots. Conditions and seeds
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:165: trailing whitespace.
+reuse episodes. The 32 remaining FULL20 fits contribute no performance
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:166: trailing whitespace.
+estimates.\ev{Extended matched evaluation scope}{\EvidenceRef{E115}{},
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:167: trailing whitespace.
+\EvidenceRef{E116}{}}

00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:254: trailing whitespace.

+\paragraph{Transferred parameters and paired contrasts.}
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:274: trailing whitespace.

00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:275: trailing whitespace.

00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:276: trailing whitespace.

00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:277: trailing whitespace.

00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:278: trailing whitespace.

00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:279: trailing whitespace.

+\subsection{Calibration, Threshold Behavior, and Unfinished Robustness Scope}
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:323: trailing whitespace.
+In outer fold~0, FROZEN20 seed~42 F2 had macro AP 0.7071 and Brier score 0.1539,
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:324: trailing whitespace.
+yet balanced accuracy was 0.5 at threshold 0.5. Ranking and probability metrics thus
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:325: trailing whitespace.
+need not imply useful decisions at the fixed operating threshold; tuning it on these
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:326: trailing whitespace.
+assessment episodes would bias decision-performance estimates.\ev{Matched secondary
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:327: trailing whitespace.
+metrics}{\EvidenceRef{E095}{}, \EvidenceRef{E096}{}}
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:328: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:329: trailing whitespace.
+The retained source implements calibration as $p=\sigma(a s+b)$ on the branch's raw
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:330: trailing whitespace.
+failure logit $s$, with $a$ and $b$ fitted on C only. Under this convention a negative
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:331: trailing whitespace.
+$a$ reverses within-fit ranking. The saved matched-study report attributes negative
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:332: trailing whitespace.
+visual slopes to outer fold~0 in every FROZEN20 and DONOR20 seed, and to FULL20
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:333: trailing whitespace.
+seed~42. Table~\ref{tab:vif-calibration} gives those reported coefficients and paired
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:334: trailing whitespace.
+raw/calibrated visual macro APs. FROZEN20 and DONOR20 outer folds~1 and~2, and all
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:335: trailing whitespace.
+three hundred-epoch pilots, have positive reported slopes.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:337: trailing whitespace.

00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:388: trailing whitespace.

00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:389: trailing whitespace.

00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:390: trailing whitespace.

00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:391: trailing whitespace.

00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:392: trailing whitespace.
+adjustment. Coefficients were fixed before outer scoring, although the labels and some
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:393: trailing whitespace.

+evaluation limits}{\EvidenceRef{E101}{}}

00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/08_vifailback.tex:397: trailing whitespace.

00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/09_discussion.tex:4: trailing whitespace.
+The welding proposal asked how a process-aware multimodal architecture could be designed
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/09_discussion.tex:5: trailing whitespace.
+and evaluated for industrial anomaly detection. It called for a comparison of what the
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/09_discussion.tex:6: trailing whitespace.
+camera, acoustic signal, and machine measurements could predict about weld quality. The
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/09_discussion.tex:7: trailing whitespace.
+project moved to PHM gear monitoring and REASSEMBLE robotic assembly. When the Intel
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/09_discussion.tex:8: trailing whitespace.
+Welding data were subsequently reconsidered, the feasibility audit documented prior use
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/09_discussion.tex:9: trailing whitespace.
+of the proposed test population (Chapter~1 and Appendix~\ref{app:chronology}). That
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/09_discussion.tex:10: trailing whitespace.
+population could no longer supply an independent estimate of weld-quality performance.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/09_discussion.tex:11: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/09_discussion.tex:12: trailing whitespace.
+Combining predictions improved failure ranking over the sensor component in REASSEMBLE
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/09_discussion.tex:13: trailing whitespace.
+assembly. The comparison used a common action outcome and held out entire recordings. In
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/09_discussion.tex:14: trailing whitespace.
+MSM milling and ViFailback manipulation, stacking ranked outcomes less accurately than
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/09_discussion.tex:15: trailing whitespace.
+its sensor component. In HANDOVER, equal averaging likewise fell below sensor-only
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/09_discussion.tex:16: trailing whitespace.

00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/09_discussion.tex:17: trailing whitespace.
+photograph-derived measure corresponded to gear damage. Each comparison used the same
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/09_discussion.tex:18: trailing whitespace.

00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/09_discussion.tex:19: trailing whitespace.

00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/09_discussion.tex:20: trailing whitespace.

00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/09_discussion.tex:361: trailing whitespace.
+Three features of the data limit a causal interpretation of architectural differences.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/09_discussion.tex:362: trailing whitespace.
+First, the target may only approximate the phenomenon of interest. PHM has no
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/09_discussion.tex:363: trailing whitespace.
+independent quantitative tooth-damage reference, MSM's label mapping defines which
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/09_discussion.tex:364: trailing whitespace.
+intervals count as abnormal, and some robotic outcomes remained ambiguous under visual
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/09_discussion.tex:365: trailing whitespace.
+review. Second, the number of independently acquired units can be much smaller than the
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/09_discussion.tex:366: trailing whitespace.
+number of predictions. Gear experiments, recording sessions, dates, tasks, and
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/09_discussion.tex:367: trailing whitespace.
+participants capture different sources of dependence. Where identifiers were missing,
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/09_discussion.tex:368: trailing whitespace.
+independence across those groups could not be verified. Third, several later analyses
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/09_discussion.tex:369: trailing whitespace.
+revisited assessment populations examined during development. Intervals conditional on
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/09_discussion.tex:370: trailing whitespace.
+fitted predictions and variation over a few seeds do not capture that selection history.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/09_discussion.tex:371: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/09_discussion.tex:372: trailing whitespace.
+Preprocessing before the separate four-arm PHM loop limits attribution of its results to
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/09_discussion.tex:373: trailing whitespace.
+architecture. In other experiments, differences in calibration, capacity, training
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/09_discussion.tex:374: trailing whitespace.
+budget or corruption exposure accompanied architectural changes. Some historical
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/09_discussion.tex:375: trailing whitespace.
+configurations lack the detail needed to reconstruct training exactly. ViFailback's
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/09_discussion.tex:376: trailing whitespace.
+complete frozen/donor matrix is supplemented by the November 24 one-fold FULL20
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/09_discussion.tex:377: trailing whitespace.
+comparison and FULL100 pilot. Their adaptation effects remain inconclusive; the
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/09_discussion.tex:378: trailing whitespace.
+remaining adaptation fits and corruption study are unexecuted (Chapter~8). The
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/09_discussion.tex:379: trailing whitespace.
+literature synthesis covers the full texts available to a single reviewer.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/09_discussion.tex:380: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/09_discussion.tex:381: trailing whitespace.
+Finally, the completed-window studies allowed the predictor to observe evidence near the
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/09_discussion.tex:382: trailing whitespace.
+outcome. Synthetic blur, noise, or stream removal test specified interventions. They
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/09_discussion.tex:383: trailing whitespace.
+give limited information about gradual calibration drift, unobserved timing faults, or
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/09_discussion.tex:384: trailing whitespace.
+shifts to another physical machine. Transferring a predictor to an earlier decision time
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/09_discussion.tex:385: trailing whitespace.
+or a different installation requires observations at that time and place.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/09_discussion.tex:388: trailing whitespace.
+In a monitoring application, the intended decision determines what constitutes failure,
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/09_discussion.tex:389: trailing whitespace.
+how it can be confirmed independently, and which observations arrive in time to be
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/09_discussion.tex:390: trailing whitespace.
+useful. Evaluating each stream on the same held-out units establishes what it can
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/09_discussion.tex:391: trailing whitespace.
+predict alone. Equal averaging and a fitted static combination then provide references
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/09_discussion.tex:392: trailing whitespace.
+for testing whether context-dependent weights add value. When an instrument declares an
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/09_discussion.tex:393: trailing whitespace.
+outage, the remaining predictor supplies a natural reference. When faulty values still
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/09_discussion.tex:394: trailing whitespace.
+appear plausible, the relevant test is whether the gate recognizes situations in which
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/09_discussion.tex:395: trailing whitespace.
+trusting that stream would worsen the prediction.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/09_discussion.tex:396: trailing whitespace.
+
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/09_discussion.tex:397: trailing whitespace.
+A prospective return to welding could reserve whole welding sessions, collect an
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/09_discussion.tex:398: trailing whitespace.
+independent weld-quality measure, and fix the prediction horizon before examining those
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/09_discussion.tex:399: trailing whitespace.
+outcomes. On the same welds, exposure- and calibration-matched models could be assessed
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/09_discussion.tex:400: trailing whitespace.
+for clean performance, known absence, degraded readings, timing error, and shifts
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/09_discussion.tex:401: trailing whitespace.
+between operating conditions. Paired effects and a prespecified tolerance for
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/09_discussion.tex:402: trailing whitespace.
+clean-performance loss would clarify the operational cost of any robustness gain. Such a
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/09_discussion.tex:403: trailing whitespace.
+test would show whether the modest gain for completed assembly actions carries over to
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/09_discussion.tex:404: trailing whitespace.
+weld-quality decisions.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/10_conclusion.tex:5: trailing whitespace.
+The study began with two questions about weld quality. Could a camera reveal changes
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/10_conclusion.tex:6: trailing whitespace.
+that machine signals missed? Would the combined prediction remain reliable if either
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/10_conclusion.tex:7: trailing whitespace.
+input deteriorated? The architecture comparison extended to gear monitoring and robotic
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/10_conclusion.tex:8: trailing whitespace.
+assembly. By the time the Intel Welding data were re-examined, development had already
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/10_conclusion.tex:9: trailing whitespace.
+drawn on the proposed test recordings. Independent welding validation would require a
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/10_conclusion.tex:10: trailing whitespace.
+new set of welds. The five studies evaluated related questions against the outcomes
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/10_conclusion.tex:11: trailing whitespace.
+available in each dataset (Appendix~\ref{app:chronology}).
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/10_conclusion.tex:13: trailing whitespace.
+In PHM, post-run photographs supplied a continuous measure of candidate damage area on
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/10_conclusion.tex:14: trailing whitespace.
+gear teeth. The photographs defined the target for sensor regression. Across held-out
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/10_conclusion.tex:15: trailing whitespace.
+experiments, combining three sensor groups gave a mean absolute error of 0.824
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/10_conclusion.tex:16: trailing whitespace.
+percentage points, compared with 0.804 for predicting the training mean
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/10_conclusion.tex:17: trailing whitespace.
+(Table~\ref{tab:phm-loeo}). The paired interval allowed either model to have lower
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/10_conclusion.tex:18: trailing whitespace.
+error. Even an accurate prediction of this proxy would still require independent
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/10_conclusion.tex:19: trailing whitespace.
+measurements to establish how closely the photographed area tracked physical damage.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/10_conclusion.tex:21: trailing whitespace.
+REASSEMBLE made a direct fusion comparison possible. Video and machine signals covered
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/10_conclusion.tex:22: trailing whitespace.
+the same completed action, each action had a recorded outcome, and entire recordings
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/10_conclusion.tex:23: trailing whitespace.
+could be held out for assessment. Across 4,530 actions from 148 recordings, logistic
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/10_conclusion.tex:24: trailing whitespace.
+stacking achieved failure average precision (AP) of .447, compared with .429 for its
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/10_conclusion.tex:25: trailing whitespace.
+sensor component and .446 for equal probability averaging
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/10_conclusion.tex:26: trailing whitespace.
+(Tables~\ref{tab:reassemble-unimodal} and~\ref{tab:reassemble-fusion}). The paired
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/10_conclusion.tex:27: trailing whitespace.
+increment over sensors was .0179 (95\% recording-bootstrap CI [.0086, .0283]); the
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/10_conclusion.tex:28: trailing whitespace.
+increment over averaging, .0016 [$-.0076$, .0112], remained unresolved. Independent
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/10_conclusion.tex:29: trailing whitespace.
+saved-score analysis verifies that vision improves within-fold AUROC in all five folds
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/10_conclusion.tex:30: trailing whitespace.
+and AP in four, beyond strictly increasing recalibration of the same sensor score.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/10_conclusion.tex:31: trailing whitespace.
+Cross-fold score alignment and the absent refitted sensor-only second-stage control
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/10_conclusion.tex:32: trailing whitespace.
+still limit attribution of the full pooled gain. These intervals condition on the saved
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/10_conclusion.tex:33: trailing whitespace.
+fits, not the complete selection process.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/10_conclusion.tex:35: trailing whitespace.
+The external studies tested whether the same finding held for different targets. In MSM,
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/10_conclusion.tex:36: trailing whitespace.
+stacking ranked abnormal milling intervals less accurately than the logistic sensor
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/10_conclusion.tex:37: trailing whitespace.
+model, with mean abnormal-cutting AP of .757 and .829, respectively; the paired
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/10_conclusion.tex:38: trailing whitespace.
+difference was $-.0717$ [$-.1004$, $-.0515$] conditional on five date clusters
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/10_conclusion.tex:39: trailing whitespace.
+(Table~\ref{tab:msm-clean}). In ViFailback, the target was the outcome of a whole
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/10_conclusion.tex:40: trailing whitespace.
+manipulation episode. Across three seeds, stacking yielded macro-AP of .621--.629
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/10_conclusion.tex:41: trailing whitespace.
+against .649 for logistic sensing. Initializing the visual encoder from REASSEMBLE gave
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/10_conclusion.tex:42: trailing whitespace.
+mixed results across the matched runs, while the completed November 24 one-fold
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/10_conclusion.tex:43: trailing whitespace.
+target-adaptation contrasts remained inconclusive (Tables~\ref{tab:vif-bothclass},
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/10_conclusion.tex:44: trailing whitespace.
+\ref{tab:vif-matched} and~\ref{tab:vif-adaptation}). In HANDOVER, averaging video and
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/10_conclusion.tex:45: trailing whitespace.
+force--torque predictions on 151 trials yielded mean failure AP of .803, below the
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/10_conclusion.tex:46: trailing whitespace.
+sensor component at .824 (Table~\ref{tab:handover-clean}). Participant-to-trial
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/10_conclusion.tex:47: trailing whitespace.
+identifiers were unavailable, so the supplementary study could not construct the
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/10_conclusion.tex:48: trailing whitespace.
+participant-disjoint predictions needed to train and evaluate its proposed stacker.
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/10_conclusion.tex:50: trailing whitespace.
+Taken together, the studies motivate modular evaluation: measure each stream's
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/10_conclusion.tex:51: trailing whitespace.
+contribution before combining predictions. This is a design recommendation supported by
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/10_conclusion.tex:52: trailing whitespace.
+contrasting outcomes, not experimentally demonstrated superiority of a single modular
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/10_conclusion.tex:53: trailing whitespace.
+architecture. The value of that combination depended on the target, the available
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/10_conclusion.tex:54: trailing whitespace.
+observations, and the strength of the sensor predictor. The results concern
00_thesis_writing_tracking/Thesis/LaTeX_Project/chapters/10_conclusion.tex:55: trailing whitespace.
+gear-condition estimation and retrospective classification of completed operations.
